### [40] [Task-Space Imitation Guidance for Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07527)
*Salar Asayesh,Hossein Darani,Todd Cao,Evgeny Andriash,Mani Ranjbar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TIGER框架，将动作分块模仿策略用作任务空间进度估计器来构建稠密奖励并指导预训练，从而提升稀疏奖励桌面机器人操作的强化学习效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励下的桌面机器人操作强化学习样本效率低，且早期在线探索容易偏离流形、产生不安全动作。现有方法多把模仿策略当作可执行控制器或动作先验，未能充分利用其提供局部进度信息的能力。

Method: TIGER不把动作分块模仿策略当作控制器或动作先验，而是作为局部任务空间进度估计器：借助控制器感知的动作到运动映射，将预测的动作分块转换为短时域末端执行器参考，据此给RL代理提供朝参考前进的稠密进度奖励，同时稀疏环境奖励仍为主导目标。预训练阶段使用模仿引导的前瞻信号，对预测能带来任务空间进展的动作放宽保守价值惩罚，以减少早期在线RL的离流形探索。

Result: 在仿真和真机实验中，TIGER提升了早期样本效率，减少了实测安全违规次数，并在所评估任务上达到或超过先前RL及IL-RL基线的最终成功率。

Conclusion: 将模仿策略重新定位为任务空间进度估计器，可用于奖励构造与预训练引导，在不牺牲最终成功率的前提下改善稀疏奖励机器人操作RL的样本效率与安全性。

Abstract: We introduce Task-Space Imitation Guidance for Efficient Reinforcement Learning (TIGER), a reward-construction and pretraining framework for sparse-reward tabletop robotic manipulation. TIGER treats an action-chunked imitation policy not as an executable controller or action prior, but as a local task-space progress estimator: predicted action chunks are converted, using controller-aware action-to-motion mapping, into short-horizon end-effector references, and the RL agent receives dense progress rewards toward these references while the sparse environment reward remains the dominant objective. During pretraining, TIGER uses imitation-guided look-ahead signals to relax conservative value penalties for actions predicted to make task-space progress, reducing off-manifold exploration during early online RL. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, TIGER improves early sample efficiency and reduces measured safety violations while matching or improving final success rates relative to prior RL and IL-RL baselines on the evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [41] [Seeing the Invisible: Physics-Guided Visual Prompting for Temperature- and Radiation-Aware VLA Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07558)
*Hojoon Son,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的物理引导视觉提示模块PG-VP，让冻结的VLA导航模型通过动态虚拟障碍物规避不可见风险（辐射、高温），无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全敏感设施中，RGB相机无法探测辐射或温度骤升等不可见风险；而传统方法为每种风险都需新增编码器、数据和重新训练模型，成本高昂。因此希望复用冻结VLA模型已有的可见障碍物规避能力来应对不可见风险。

Method: PG-VP对近端辐射或热源进行物理引导的风险评估，确定规避方向，并叠加一个随连续帧移动的对应虚拟障碍物（动态视觉提示）。导航策略据此自然绕开该不可见危险。无论风险类型如何都使用相同的虚拟障碍物，因此视觉提示模式在增加传感器时保持固定；未检测到风险时不渲染任何内容，策略行为与无PG-VP时完全一致。

Result: 在OmniNav上以R2R-CE和RxR-CE的val-unseen划分评估，PG-VP分别有84.9%和83.2%的情况引导策略趋向预期的低风险动作，导航成功率代价为6.8和7.9个百分点。在真实机器人上面对实际热源和辐射源的场景测试中，无需任何重训练，最差10%平均轨迹安全性针对热源和辐射源分别提升63.45%和32.59%。

Conclusion: PG-VP是一种无需重训练、即插即用的多模态感知模块，能有效复用冻结VLA模型能力规避不可见风险，在仿真与真实机器人上均验证了其安全增益。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a major paradigm for Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN). However, in safety-critical facilities, invisible risks such as radiation or temperature spikes cannot be detected by an RGB camera, and handling each risk is expensive, requiring a new encoder, new data, and model retraining. We propose Physics-Guided Visual Prompting (PG-VP), a plug-and-play multimodal perception module that instead reuses what a frozen VLA model already does well: avoiding visible obstacles. Given a proximal radiation or thermal source, PG-VP performs a physics-guided risk assessment to determine the avoidance direction and overlays a corresponding virtual obstacle that moves across consecutive frames (Dynamic Visual Prompting). The navigation policy then naturally detours around this invisible hazard. The identical virtual obstacle is used regardless of hazard type, so the visual prompting pattern remains fixed as sensors are added. When no hazard is detected, nothing is rendered, and the policy behaves exactly as it would without PG-VP. We evaluate PG-VP on OmniNav using the val-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, where it guides the policy toward intended low-risk actions in 84.9% and 83.2% of cases, at a cost of 6.8 and 7.9 percentage points in navigation success rate. We further test it with distinct scenarios on a real robot in the presence of actual thermal and radiation sources, all without any retraining. The real test shows that PG-VP effectively avoids these invisible hazards, improving worst-10% average trajectory safety by 63.45% and 32.59% against thermal and radiation sources, respectively.

</details>


### [42] [OpenSplatGraph: From Dense Semantic Maps to Structured Scene Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07569)
*Binh Long Nguyen,Kien Nguyen,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSplatGraph：从在线高斯式开放词汇语义地图直接构建持久3D场景图的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集3D建图对复杂环境机器人感知至关重要。现有3D高斯泼溅建图虽能实现高保真几何与开放词汇感知，但语义表示为非结构化特征场，限制了以对象为中心的推理；而3D场景图虽能显式建模对象及关系，却多基于稀疏几何表示构建，未充分利用密集语义地图。

Method: 提出统一框架OpenSplatGraph，在密集语义地图上引入可靠性感知语义场，维护轻量观测统计，实现置信度感知、查询条件下的对象提取；将提取的实例关联到持久图节点，使对象属性与关系可随观测和查询增量更新；将密集语义建图与持久对象中心表示紧耦合。

Result: 在标准3D场景理解基准及真实机器人实验中，该框架在在线开放词汇感知与下游机器人任务上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 紧耦合密集语义建图与持久对象中心表示，可同时支持语言引导的对象定位与结构化关系推理，并保持高斯建图的几何保真度。

Abstract: Dense 3D mapping with semantic understanding is essential for robotic perception in complex environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting-based mapping approaches enable high-fidelity geometry and efficient open-vocabulary perception, but typically represent semantics as unstructured feature fields that limit object-centric reasoning. In contrast, 3D scene graphs explicitly model objects and their relationships for structured reasoning, but are commonly constructed from sparse geometric representations that do not fully exploit dense semantic maps. In this work, we present OpenSplatGraph, a unified framework that constructs persistent 3D scene graphs directly from an online Gaussian-based open-vocabulary semantic map. The proposed framework augments the dense semantic map with a reliability-aware semantic field that maintains lightweight observation statistics for confidence-aware, query-conditioned object extraction. Extracted object instances are associated with persistent graph nodes, allowing object attributes and relationships to be incrementally updated across observations and queries. By tightly coupling dense semantic mapping with persistent object-centric representations, our framework supports both language-guided object grounding and structured relational reasoning while preserving the geometric fidelity of Gaussian-based mapping. Comprehensive evaluations on standard 3D scene understanding benchmarks and real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that OpenSplatGraph achieves competitive performance for online open-vocabulary perception and downstream robotic tasks. Project page: https://csiro-robotics.github.io/OpenSplatGraph.

</details>


### [43] [BiGym 2.0: Benchmarking Learned and Agent-Developed Policies for Humanoid Household Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07594)
*Zexi Zhang,Zecheng Zhu,Zidong Chen,Zulkhuu Tuya,Stephen James*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiGym 2.0 将 BiGym 适配到 Unitree G1 人形机器人，覆盖 20 项家务任务，使用统一全身控制器进行演示与评测，并提供每任务 60 条人类 VR 演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人家务操作需要手臂动作与身体平衡、迈步、姿态变换协同，现有基准缺乏针对全身控制的人形家务评测平台与配套人类演示。

Method: 基于 Unitree G1 构建 20 项家务任务套件，采用统一全身控制器进行演示采集与评估；每任务提供 60 条原生人类 VR 演示，含同步多相机视角与全身执行记录；在同一观测、本体感受与控制器条件下评测 VLA 微调、模仿学习、演示驱动强化学习以及冷启动编码智能体。

Result: 在九任务均值上，视觉-语言-动作（VLA）微调表现最佳；智能体开发的程序优于所有演示驱动强化学习基线，并在双臂抓取上领先。跨工作空间堆叠仍未被解决，π0.5 在 pick-box 上表现偏低，多物体运输对模仿学习、演示驱动强化学习和编码智能体均较困难。

Conclusion: BiGym 2.0 提供了人形全身家务操作的统一基准与人类演示资源，揭示了 VLA 微调与程序化智能体的相对优势，同时指出跨空间堆叠、pick-box 与多物体运输等开放难题；环境、演示与评测轨迹均已开源。

Abstract: Humanoid household manipulation requires the arms to act while the body balances, steps and changes posture. We present BiGym 2.0, an adaptation of BiGym for the Unitree G1 across 20 household tasks using a unified whole-body controller for demonstration and evaluation. The suite provides 60 native human virtual-reality demonstrations per task with synchronised multi-camera views and full-body execution records. We benchmark vision-language-action fine-tuning, imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning, and cold-start coding agents given the interaction budget of online reinforcement learning. With the same onboard views, proprioception and whole-body controller for every method, vision-language-action fine-tuning has the highest nine-task mean, and agent-developed programs outperform every demo-driven reinforcement learning baseline on this mean and lead on bimanual reaching. Cross-workspace stacking remains open, $π_{0.5}$ stays low on pick-box, and multi-object transport is hard for imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning and coding agents. All environments, human demonstrations, and evaluation traces are open-sourced at https://github.com/swirl-uk/BiGym2.

</details>


### [44] [The Robot Is Not Its Description: GaugeBench for Representation Robustness in Morphology-Aware Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07597)
*Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,Rajesh K. Gupta,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 GaugeBench 基准，发现机器人描述约定（如关节轴方向）的等价改写会导致形态感知策略性能崩溃，其破坏性甚至超过更换机器人本体，揭示了表征鲁棒性与机制鲁棒性的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态感知策略依赖机器人描述构建的接口，而跨具身评估通常固定描述约定、只改变机器人本体，忽略了一个问题：当机器人不变、仅其描述（如关节轴方向、关节角零点、连杆/关节顺序与命名）被物理等价地改写时，策略行为是否还能存活。作者希望隔离并量化这种描述变化带来的影响。

Method: 提出 GaugeBench：在保持物理与策略接口不变的前提下，用物理等价的约定重写固定机械结构，并评估同一策略权重；对 MetaMorph、ModuMorph 及一个无关的 PyBullet 框架进行测试；通过消融（轴反转、关节角零点变化、重排序）定位失败来源；并尝试精确双描述传输与跨等价轴约定的训练来修复。

Result: MetaMorph 策略在 80 个熟悉机器人上得分 4030.6，但等价重描述后仅 51.6，而 98 个真正留出的机器人得 1489.6；新描述比新机器人更具破坏性。消融显示轴反转即可复现崩溃，关节角零点变化几乎无害，重排序介于两者之间；仅改变关节状态与力矩坐标即可导致失败，而仅改变描述衍生特征则不会。该现象也出现在 ModuMorph 与 PyBullet 框架中。精确双描述传输可恢复原控制器，跨等价轴约定训练可将轴反转下的保留回报从 3.6% 提升至 80.6%。

Conclusion: 研究区分了机制鲁棒性与表征鲁棒性，表明跨具身评估应同时测试两者；描述约定的等价改写是可修复的，为更可靠的跨具身泛化评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: A robot description does more than specify a physical mechanism: it also encodes arbitrary conventions, such as joint-axis direction, joint-angle zero, and the order and names of links and joints. Morphology-aware policies consume interfaces built from these descriptions, yet cross-embodiment evaluation typically changes the robot while keeping those conventions fixed. This leaves a simple question unanswered: does behavior survive when the robot stays fixed but its description changes? GaugeBench isolates this case by rewriting a fixed mechanism under physically equivalent conventions, verifying that its physics and policy interface are preserved, and then evaluating the same policy weights. The result is stark: three MetaMorph policies score 4030.6 on 80 familiar robots, but only 51.6 when those same robots are equivalently re-described, while 98 genuinely held-out robots score 1489.6. A new description can therefore be more damaging than a new robot. Tracing the failure reveals that axis reversal alone reproduces the collapse, joint-angle zero changes are nearly harmless, and reordering lies between them; moreover, changing joint-state and torque coordinates alone is sufficient to cause the failure, while changing description-derived features alone is not. The same phenomenon appears in ModuMorph and an unrelated PyBullet framework. Yet it is not irreversible: exact two-description transport restores the original controller, and training across equivalent axis conventions raises retained return under axis reversal from 3.6% to 80.6%. Together, these results separate mechanism robustness from representation robustness and show that cross-embodiment evaluation should test both.

</details>


### [45] [Modeling Latent Disturbances for Robust Decision-Making in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07599)
*Junwon Seo,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在世界模型（WM）潜空间中实现鲁棒决策，通过将潜空间扰动建模为对学习到的潜动力学的扰动来诱导悲观但合理的转移，并用共形预测校准不确定性集，进而通过博弈论优化联合求解鲁棒动作与最坏情况扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化需要显式指定的动力学和有物理意义的扰动，才能选出在最坏扰动下仍有效的动作。但世界模型的状态空间和动力学是从高维观测中完全学习得到的，因此如何定义能够忠实反映底层系统不确定性的潜空间扰动成为一个根本性挑战。

Method: 核心思想是将潜空间扰动建模为对学习到的潜动力学的扰动，从而诱导悲观但合理的转移。具体做法是结合动力学感知的相似性度量（捕捉合理转移）与分布外检测（排除不合理潜状态），构建一组合理的潜动力学；再用共形预测对该不确定性集进行校准，确保由潜扰动诱导的 WM 想象保持合理而不至于过度悲观；最后通过博弈论优化联合优化鲁棒机器人动作与最坏情况潜扰动，并将其用于鲁棒化策略引导（潜空间安全滤波与生成式控制策略的采样-验证引导两种范式）。

Result: 在受控仿真实验中，所提潜扰动能够直接在世界模型潜空间中实现鲁棒决策；在 Franka 机械臂的硬件实验中，建模潜扰动实现了鲁棒的策略引导，安全滤波将失败减少 70%，基于采样的策略引导将失败减少 54%。

Conclusion: 通过合理定义并校准潜空间扰动，可以在世界模型的潜空间中直接进行鲁棒决策，并在真实机器人任务上显著提升策略鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study robust decision-making in the latent space of world models (WMs). Robust optimization is a mathematical framework where, given explicitly specified dynamics and physically meaningful disturbances, a robot can select actions that remain effective even under worst-case disturbances. However, applying this principle to the learned latent space of WMs introduces a fundamental challenge: because WMs have fully learned state spaces and dynamics inferred from high-dimensional observations, it is unclear how to define latent-space disturbances that faithfully represent uncertainty in the underlying system. Our key idea is to model a latent-space disturbance as a perturbation to the learned latent dynamics that induces pessimistic but plausible transitions. Specifically, we construct a set of plausible latent dynamics by combining a dynamics-aware similarity metric that captures plausible transitions with out-of-distribution detection that excludes implausible latent states. We calibrate this uncertainty set over latent dynamics using conformal prediction, ensuring that WM imaginations induced by the latent disturbance remain plausible without becoming overly pessimistic. We then jointly optimize robust robot actions and the worst-case latent disturbances through game-theoretic optimization. We leverage this latent-space robust optimization to robustify policy steering, considering two paradigms: latent safety filtering and sample-and-verify steering of a generative control policy. Our controlled simulation experiments show that our latent disturbance enables robust decision-making directly in WM latent spaces, and hardware experiments with a Franka manipulator show that modeling latent disturbances enables robust policy steering, reducing failures by 70% in safety filtering and 54% in sampling-based policy steering. Project website: https://junwon.me/LatentDisturbance/.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Grasp Targeting from Point Clouds for Log Pile Clearing on a Hydraulic Crane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07613)
*George Sideris,Lucas Bessai,Heshan Fernando,Elie Ayoub,Nicolas Lemieux,Inna Sharf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了木材堆场中木材装载机的抓取策略学习，提出了一个统一的网络来同时支持行为克隆（BC）、强化学习（RL）和部署，并在仿真和真实林业起重机上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在木材堆场中，原木堆密集且相互接触，每次抓取都会改变后续堆型，传统方法难以应对。作者希望用学习策略从未分割点云中决定抓斗的位置和方向，并在真实设备上部署。

Method: 使用统一网络对观测点进行分类并预测深度和抓斗方向，支持BC、RL及BC→RL微调。BC从成功堆顶演示中学习，RL通过微调或从头训练探索改进。在仿真和牵引式液压林业起重机上进行了12次现场试验，对比几何启发式、从头RL、BC和BC→RL。

Result: 仿真中BC清空98/100个200根原木堆，BC→RL提升了负载稳定性。现场试验中BC和BC→RL分别转移了93.8%和88.9%的总库存，优于启发式的80.4%；BC→RL在83.6%的循环中成功放置原木，高于启发式的79.6%和BC的65.7%，但仿真稳定性增益未迁移到真实设备。学习策略在未滤波点云上仍优于手工滤波启发式。

Conclusion: 完全在仿真中训练、在起重机上原样运行的学习策略，能够在包含存储架轨道和立柱的未滤波点云上清空比手工滤波启发式更多的原木，展示了统一BC/RL网络在真实林业作业中的可行性。

Abstract: In mill yards, log loaders clear dense piles by a sequence of bundle grasps: hundreds of logs rest in contact, and each removal changes the pile available to the next grasp. A learned policy chooses where to place and orient the grapple from unsegmented point clouds and runs on a trailer-mounted hydraulic forestry crane. The policy classifies at which observed point to grasp and predicts depth and grapple orientation there. The same network outputs support behavior cloning (BC), reinforcement learning (RL), and deployment. BC learns from successful top-of-pile demonstrations; RL explores for improvements by fine-tuning the cloned policy (BC$\to$RL) or by training from scratch. In simulation, BC clears 98 of 100 piles of 200 logs, while BC$\to$RL improves load stability. Twelve field trials compare a geometric heuristic, RL from scratch, BC, and BC$\to$RL through complete grasp-transport-deposit cycles. BC and BC$\to$RL deposit 93.8% and 88.9% of pooled inventory, against 80.4% for the heuristic. BC$\to$RL deposits logs on 83.6% of its cycles, against 79.6% for the heuristic and 65.7% for BC, while its simulated stability gain does not carry over to the crane testbed. Trained entirely in simulation and run unchanged on the crane, the learned policies clear more than the hand-filtered heuristic while observing unfiltered clouds that still contain the storage rack's rails and poles.

</details>


### [47] [Nine Trials to Recover: A Reproducible Benchmark for Repertoire-Free Soft-Robot Damage Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07616)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个无动作库的软体机器人损伤恢复基准，采用九次在线试验协议，并给出GP-EI参考控制器，在两组75个身体队列上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人损伤恢复缺乏可复用、无动作库且能公平比较学习式损伤先验与适应方法的基准，现有评测常混淆方法开发与新身体评估。

Method: 构建无repertoire基准：形态内配对损伤掩码、保留实测标称回退、开发与确认分离；提出高斯过程期望改进（GP-EI）参考控制器，用三个初始化探测和六个反馈选择rollout调整执行器相位；与随机搜索、Sobol、CEM、CMA-ES及TuRBO风格局部GP在等预算下比较。

Result: GP-EI在69/75个确认身体上提升最差掩码增益，平均最差掩码奖励高出四个基线0.235-0.310；与TuRBO风格局部GP的配对置信区间含零；固定控制器回放平均恢复5.06体素宽度，但最差掩码p99几何边缘应变增加0.0031，严格无新增需求部署门保留48.7%平均增益；冻结开发压力测试中GP-EI提升71/75身体，平均最差掩码增益高于官方BoTorch TuRBO 0.356。

Conclusion: 可回放控制器、身体级推断与冻结队列评估为衡量学习式损伤先验及未来自适应方法的价值提供了参考。

Abstract: We present a repertoire-free benchmark for soft-robot damage recovery under nine online trials. The protocol pairs damage masks within each morphology, retains a measured nominal fallback, and separates method development from evaluation on new bodies. A Gaussian-process expected-improvement (GP-EI) reference controller adapts actuator phases using three initialization probes and six feedback-selected rollouts. Across two disjoint 75-body cohorts, it outperforms random search, Sobol, CEM, and CMA-ES under equal budgets. GP-EI improves the worst-mask gain on 69/75 confirmation bodies and exceeds these four baselines by 0.235-0.310 mean worst-mask reward. A TuRBO-style local GP is the closest comparator; the paired confidence interval includes zero. Post-confirmation fixed-controller replay finds 5.06 voxel widths of mean recovery together with a 0.0031 increase in worst-mask p99 geometric edge strain; a strict no-added-demand deployment gate retains 48.7% of mean gain. In a frozen development stress test that physically removes 10% of occupied voxels, GP-EI improves 71/75 bodies and exceeds official BoTorch TuRBO by 0.356 mean worst-mask gain. Together, the replayable controllers, body-level inference, and frozen-cohort evaluation provide a reference for measuring the added value of learned damage priors and future adaptation methods.

</details>


### [48] [TacZero: Training-Free Peg Insertion Using a General-Purpose Vision-Language Model with Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07621)
*Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacZero，直接用预训练通用视觉语言模型（VLM）解读视觉与触觉观测并选择机器人动作，无需额外触觉/操作训练或任务专用规则，在圆柱插销任务中取得 15/20 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要从语言指令与感官观测自主决定动作，以完成新的富接触操作任务，而无需任务专用训练或人工规则。这类任务要求机器人推断物体间如何接触及由此产生的运动，再选择动作。已有方法要么依赖设计估计模型与触觉反馈控制律，要么针对物体运动估计、动作结果预测与动作选择学习专门模型，成本高且难以泛化。

Method: TacZero 利用预训练通用视觉语言模型（VLM）解读视觉与触觉观测并选择机器人动作，不进行额外触觉或操作训练，也不使用任务专用规则。它向 VLM 输入相机图像、机器人状态，以及以数值或叠加在图像上的向量形式表示的三轴触觉响应；VLM 依据这些观测与交互历史生成指令，指定目标末端执行器位置以及夹爪张开或闭合，由底层控制器执行。

Result: 在真实世界圆柱插销实验中，TacZero 使用数值触觉输入时 20 次试验成功 15 次；不使用触觉输入时为 20 次成功 10 次。这表明触觉信息的引入显著提升了表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为使用通用 VLM 进行富接触操作提供了具体的起点，并指出了沿此方向推进所面临的挑战。

Abstract: Robots that autonomously determine their actions from language instructions and sensory observations could perform new contact-rich manipulation tasks without task-specific training or hand-designed rules. To perform these tasks, robots must infer how objects contact one another and move as a result, then select actions. For contact inference and action selection, prior approaches involve designing estimation models and tactile feedback control laws, or learning models for object-motion estimation, action-outcome prediction, and action selection from tactile data. Instead, we propose TacZero, which uses a pretrained general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) to interpret visual and tactile observations and select robot actions without additional tactile or manipulation training or task-specific rules for contact interpretation or action selection. TacZero provides the VLM with camera images, robot state, and three-axis tactile responses represented as numerical values or vectors overlaid on the images. From these observations and interaction history, the VLM generates commands specifying target end-effector positions and gripper opening or closing, which a low-level controller executes. In real-world cylindrical-peg insertion experiments, TacZero succeeded in 15 of 20 trials with numerical tactile input, compared with 10 of 20 without tactile input. This study provides a concrete starting point for further research on contact-rich manipulation using general-purpose VLMs and highlights challenges in pursuing this direction.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Task Reward: A Controller-Restriction Protocol for Evaluating Embodiment-Dependent Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07629)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用"受限控制器下的任务能力"来刻画共设计形态，而非仅用任务奖励，并系统检验其可靠性、对控制器族的依赖及作为搜索目标的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共设计方法通常用一个数字（充分优化后的任务奖励）评判机器人身体与控制器，但该数字无法区分那些对控制器依赖程度差异很大的形态，因此需要一个更能反映形态自身属性的评价指标。

Method: 在明确声明的低复杂度控制器族、环境、任务和搜索预算下，限制控制器并记录形态保留的任务能力；在三个EvoGym运动任务上分析该指标与任务奖励的方差解释关系、几何描述符的预测性、跨优化器重启的可靠性、对控制器族的依赖，并测试其作为第二搜索目标及奖励搜索后筛选指标的用途。

Result: 任务奖励仅解释该指标方差的39%、33%和10%；几何描述符在分运行交叉验证下无法预测它。测量跨优化器重启可靠（ICC(2,k)=0.956–0.986），但依赖声明的控制器族：按执行器索引相位替代位置排序时，相同形态的Spearman相关为0.50–0.63，并会反转奖励匹配的配对。作为第二搜索目标，在匹配任务奖励下5次配对运行中有3次提升能力，低于预注册的4次门槛。作为奖励搜索后的选择标准，在8次运行中均选出不同身体，代价至多0.10奖励单位，其中6次在留出族下得分更高。

Conclusion: 受限控制器下的能力是共设计形态的一种可报告属性，但只能结合定义它的控制器族来解释。

Abstract: Co-design methods optimize a robot's body and controller jointly and judge the result by one number, the task reward of the fully optimized pair. That number cannot separate morphologies whose competence depends on the controller to very different degrees. We evaluate a morphology by restricting its controller instead, recording the task competence it retains under an explicitly declared, low-complexity controller family, environment, task and search budget. On three EvoGym locomotion tasks, task reward explains only $39\%$, $33\%$ and $10\%$ of the variance in this quantity, and geometric descriptors do not predict it under run-grouped cross-validation. The measurement is reliable across optimizer restarts (ICC$(2,k) = 0.956$--$0.986$) but depends on the declared family: phasing the drive by actuator index instead of position ranks the same morphologies at Spearman $0.50$--$0.63$ and reverses reward-matched pairs. As a second search objective the axis improved competence at matched task reward in $3$ of $5$ paired runs, short of a pre-registered bar of $4$. Used after an ordinary reward-only search instead, to choose within its top task-reward band, it selected a different body in all $8$ runs offering a choice, at a cost of at most $0.10$ reward units, and in $6$ of $8$ that body also scored higher under a held-out family. Restricted-control competence is therefore a reportable property of a co-designed morphology, interpretable only with the controller family that defines it.

</details>


### [50] [OntoPlan: An Ontology-Grounded Scene Representation and Agentic Framework for Scalable Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07649)
*Hyeongwoo Nam,Woongje Cho,Juwon Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出本体论驱动的场景表示与OntoPlan智能体框架，解决大语言模型在长时程、大场景机器人任务规划中的空间理解差和token开销大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的机器人任务规划在开放式指令跟随上有前景，但在大环境的长时程任务中性能下降。文本化空间信息导致模型难以捕捉空间上下文，且token开销随环境规模增长；直接用LLM生成动作序列难以满足当前世界状态与动作前置条件。

Method: 采用本体论接地的场景表示，将物体、空间、关系和状态对齐到共享符号词表以支持空间推理与任务规划；提出OntoPlan智能体框架，负责解析指令、选择性检索任务相关信息、形式化目标与约束并生成可执行计划。

Result: 在覆盖5个室内环境和3种场景尺度的150个通用任务上，OntoPlan平均任务成功率为0.89，最强基线为0.27；平均每任务仅用18.1k token，比最高效基线少约5.6倍。随场景规模增大，其优势保持，而先前方法成功率下降更明显且token成本更高。对模糊或不可行指令能通过追问或提示信息不足来恰当响应。

Conclusion: 本体论接地的场景表示结合智能体式规划框架，可显著提升大规模长时程机器人任务规划的成功率与效率，并具备对模糊/不可行指令的稳健处理能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based robot task planning is promising for open-ended instruction following, but degrades on long-horizon tasks in large environments. When spatial information is conveyed to the LLM through text, the model can fail to capture spatial context, and token cost grows with environment size. Generating action sequences directly with an LLM also makes it difficult to satisfy the current world state and action preconditions. We address this with an ontology-grounded scene representation that aligns objects, spaces, relations, and states in a shared symbolic vocabulary for spatial reasoning and task planning, and with OntoPlan, an agentic framework that interprets instructions, selectively retrieves task-relevant information, formalizes goals and constraints, and produces executable plans. Across 150 general tasks spanning five indoor environments and three scene scales, OntoPlan achieves 0.89 average task success, compared with 0.27 for the strongest baseline, while using 18.1k total tokens per task on average, about 5.6$\times$ fewer than the most efficient baseline. These advantages persist as scene scale increases, whereas prior methods degrade more sharply in success and remain far more costly in tokens. OntoPlan also responds appropriately to ambiguous or infeasible instructions by asking follow-up questions or reporting insufficient information rather than committing to invalid plans. Code available at https://github.com/namhyeongwoo/OntoPlan.

</details>


### [51] [Silicon Language: A Robot-Native Knowledge Exchange Framework for Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07650)
*Yi Liu,Xianglin Meng,Chang Chen,Jingjing Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Silicon Language"机器人原生知识交换框架，让机器人作为能力演化的主体，通过知识包编码、发布、检索、翻译与本地试用实现异构机器人间的能力复用，并在30天概念验证部署中显示单能力适配时间从数天缩短至数小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人之间复用能力仍需大量人工适配与验证：迁移一项技能往往意味着重新设计接口、重新调参并重新验证安全性。作者希望把机器人从被动工具转变为自身能力演化的主动主体，降低跨本体知识迁移的成本与负迁移风险。

Method: 框架包含三层基础设施（边缘智能体、Silicon Transfer Protocol (STP)、知识枢纽）以及受人类学术体系启发的"能力栈"。流程为：机器人将自身经验编码为知识包并发布，检索同伴知识包，针对自身传感器与执行器进行翻译，并通过独立本地试验进行审阅。接收端设有可用性评估程序由机器人自主判断外部知识包是否有用，并采用渐进式融合与自动回滚来降低负迁移风险。验证方式为30天概念验证部署（榆林某地上模拟矿井实验室），随后在户外沙路与室内工厂地面试验；两个异构机器人经操作员辅助的文件拷贝（由Silicon Language翻译层中介）跨本体编码与翻译三项能力。

Result: 项目记录显示，单个能力的适配时间从数天缩短到数小时；扬尘锁定跟随行为的源端试验记录在Taurus上完成。作者声明这些数据为描述性部署记录，而非受控测量结果。

Conclusion: 该部署为跨本体知识交换提供了初步证据；车队级自主演化以及有/无知识包的受控对比实验仍是未来工作。

Abstract: Reusing a capability across heterogeneous robots still requires substantial human adaptation and verification: transferring a skill often means re-engineering interfaces, retuning parameters, and re-validating safety. We introduce Silicon Language, a robot-native knowledge exchange framework that treats the robot as the active subject of its own capability evolution. In this framework, a robot that wants a capability encodes its own experience into knowledge packets, publishes them, retrieves peer packets, translates them for its own sensors and actuators, and reviews them through independent local trial. A receiver-side usability evaluation procedure lets each robot decide for itself whether an external packet is useful, and progressive blending with automatic rollback is designed to reduce the risk of negative transfer when adopting it. The system combines three infrastructure layers (edge agent, Silicon Transfer Protocol (STP), and knowledge hub) with a capability stack inspired by the human scholarly system. We report a 30-day proof-of-concept deployment at an above-ground simulated-mine laboratory in Yulin, with following trials on an outdoor sand road and an indoor factory floor. Two heterogeneous robots encoded and translated three capabilities across embodiments through operator-assisted file copies mediated by the Silicon Language translation layer; source-side trials of the dust-locked following behavior were recorded on Taurus. Project records indicate that per-capability adaptation time dropped from days to hours; we present these figures as descriptive deployment records rather than controlled measurements. The deployment provides initial evidence for cross-embodiment knowledge exchange; fleet-level autonomous evolution and controlled with/without-packet comparisons remain future work.

</details>


### [52] [SMART: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Articulated Object Manipulation via Large-Scale Synthetic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07652)
*Jicong Ao,Shuhan Jiang,Yuling Zhong,Yanwen Liu,Yuhan Gao,Jiangyuan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Shiqiang Zhu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出SMART系统，通过铰接感知仿真平台SMART-Sim与分布式合成流程，生成超100万条铰接物体操作演示数据SMART-Data，用于预训练VLA模型并实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统需要与铰接物体交互的能力，但真实世界演示数据采集困难，因为涉及精确接触与约束跟随运动。仿真虽是可替代方案，但现有合成数据覆盖的铰接物体类别有限，通用合成流程缺乏对部件级语义与铰接约束的显式设计，难以支持代理式任务生成与高质量演示的可扩展合成。

Method: 提出SMART可扩展系统。核心是SMART-Sim仿真平台，采用铰接感知设计，支持有效任务生成与高效演示采集。在此基础上应用代理式任务生成，并设计可扩展分布式合成系统，合成包含超过100万条演示、44种原子任务类型、5种机器人配置、2507个铰接物体的SMART-Data数据集，并用其预训练VLA模型。

Result: 在SMART-Data上预训练的VLA模型在仿真基准上表现具有竞争力，并在真实世界铰接物体操作任务中实现零样本仿真到现实迁移和可扩展性能。

Conclusion: 合成演示能够为接触丰富的铰接物体操作提供有效且可扩展的监督信号，提升VLA模型性能，显示出该方向的潜力。

Abstract: The ability to interact with articulated objects is essential for embodied intelligent systems, but collecting large-scale real-world demonstrations for these interactions remains challenging due to the precise contact and constraint-following motions involved. Although simulation provides a promising alternative, existing synthetic data efforts cover limited articulated-object categories, while general-purpose synthesis pipelines lack explicit designs for part-level semantics and articulation constraints, hindering agentic task generation and scalable synthesis of high-quality articulated-manipulation demonstrations. To bridge this gap, we introduce SMART, a scalable system leveraging large-scale Synthesized Manipulation demonstrations for ARTiculated-object manipulation. At its core, we develop SMART-Sim, a simulation platform with articulation-aware design that enables effective task generation and efficient demonstration collection. Building on SMART-Sim, we apply agentic task generation and design a scalable distributed synthesis system, using them to synthesize SMART-Data, comprising over 1M demonstrations across 44 atomic task types, 5 robot setups, and 2,507 articulated objects. The vision-language-action (VLA) model pretrained on SMART-Data shows competitive performance on simulation benchmarks and achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and scalable performance in real-world articulated-object manipulation tasks. This highlights the potential of synthetic demonstrations in providing effective and scalable supervision for improving VLA model performance in contact-rich articulated-object manipulation.

</details>


### [53] [EigenDEXplore: Structured Exploration for Dexterous Manipulation with Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07681)
*Harsh Gupta,Tyler Ga Wei Lum,Changhao Wang,Chuer Pan,C. Karen Liu,Jeannette Bohg,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EigenDEXplore方法，通过沿人手数据导出的特征向量添加扰动来构建相关探索，在不改变动作空间的前提下显著提升灵巧操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是高维优化问题，需要多关节协调运动。强化学习与采样轨迹优化通常依赖独立关节扰动进行探索，难以发现协调行为。已有工作通过学习低维协调关节空间来降低搜索难度，但限制了泛化操作的表达能力；而结合学习动作与关节动作的方法虽恢复表达力，却增加维度并引入冗余。因此需要研究人类运动先验的最佳利用方式。

Method: 在不同操作任务中系统研究动作维度、探索策略与人类数据来源的影响。基于"人类运动先验用于结构化探索而非改变动作表示"的发现，提出EigenDEXplore：在独立关节空间噪声上叠加沿人类数据导出特征向量（eigenvectors）的扰动，从而诱导相关探索，保持动作空间不变。

Result: 在多种灵巧手上，EigenDEXplore在抓取、手中重定向和接触丰富操作任务中持续优于关节空间与学习动作空间基线。这些增益涵盖无结构与参考引导的强化学习、轨迹优化及仿真到真实部署，且在奖励塑形与课程设计较少时增益最大。

Conclusion: 人类运动先验最有效的用法是结构化探索而非改变动作表示。仅通过沿人类特征向量添加相关扰动即可在不增加维度的情况下显著提升灵巧操作的探索效率与任务表现。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation poses a challenging high-dimensional optimization problem, as useful behaviors require coordinated motion across many hand joints. In reinforcement learning (RL) and sampling-based trajectory optimization, exploration commonly relies on independent robot joint perturbations, making coordinated behaviors difficult to discover. Prior work reduces this search space for grasp learning using low-dimensional spaces of coordinated joint motions learned from human hand data, but this restricts the expressivity required for general manipulation. Some combine learned and joint-space actions to restore expressivity, but this increases dimensionality and introduces redundancy. We study these effects across diverse manipulation settings, varying action dimensionality, exploration strategy, and the source of human data. Our experiments suggest that human-motion priors are most effective when used to structure exploration rather than change the action representation. Motivated by this finding, we propose EigenDEXplore, which induces correlated exploration by adding perturbations along human-derived eigenvectors to independent joint-space noise, leaving the action space unchanged. Across multiple dexterous hands, EigenDEXplore consistently outperforms joint-space and learned action-space baselines in grasping, in-hand reorientation, and contact-rich manipulation. These gains span unstructured and reference-guided RL, trajectory optimization, and sim-to-real deployment, and are largest in settings with less reward shaping and curriculum design.

</details>


### [54] [ExoBridge: Learning a Bare Hand to Hand-Worn Exoskeleton Mapping through Human Limb Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07692)
*Ruitong Tian,Xianyao Li,Noah B. Wilson,Fang Xu,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoBridge 利用人类双手协同行为，通过外骨骼测量构建从裸手视频到动作与触觉状态的桥接函数，实现对机械手操作接口的监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是灵巧机器人学习的可扩展经验来源，但在保持裸手交互的同时获取动作和触觉监督十分困难。

Method: 采集时一只手保持裸手提供视觉观测，另一只手佩戴外骨骼提供同步的动作与触觉测量；用配对演示训练时序视觉模型，从裸手视频预测指尖接触、连续触觉强度和相对编码器运动，并通过已有标定将外骨骼运动坐标链接到灵巧机械手。

Result: 在四个操作任务的1215次演示上评估，使用留出采集会话，接触检测的汇总 AUROC 为0.916，触觉强度的 Pearson 相关系数为0.790；模型还能从裸手视频预测外骨骼构型的相对变化。

Conclusion: 人类肢体耦合可将外骨骼测量转化为裸手视频的监督信号，从而在人类视觉演示与面向机器人的操作接口之间建立学习到的桥接。

Abstract: Human video offers a scalable source of experience for dexterous robot learning, but obtaining motion and tactile supervision while preserving bare hand interaction remains challenging. We present ExoBridge, a framework that leverages human limb coupling to learn a bridging function from bare hand video to the motion and tactile state of a sensorized exoskeleton. Our central idea is to use coordinated bimanual behavior to connect an uninstrumented visual source with a measured manipulation interface. During collection, one hand remains bare and provides visual observations, while the opposite hand wears the exoskeleton and supplies synchronized motion and tactile measurements. These paired demonstrations train a temporal visual model to predict fingertip contact, continuous tactile intensity, and relative encoder motion from bare hand video alone. The exoskeleton defines an intermediate state space whose motion coordinates are linked to a dexterous robot hand through existing calibration. Evaluation on 1,215 demonstrations across four manipulation tasks uses held out collection sessions and yields a pooled any contact AUROC of 0.916 and a Pearson correlation of 0.790 for tactile intensity. The learned bridge also predicts relative changes in exoskeleton configuration from bare hand video. These results demonstrate that human limb coupling can turn exoskeleton measurements into supervision for bare hand video, establishing a learned bridge between human visual demonstrations and a robot oriented manipulation interface.

</details>


### [55] [ESP: Energy-Score Policy for One-Step Multimodal Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07696)
*Lilika Makabe,Heecheol Kim,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ESP（Energy-Score Policy）提出一种无需教师模型、单次网络评估即可生成动作块的策略，通过以能量分数替代均方误差训练动作头，实现多模态动作分布的直接学习，在仿真与真机任务中取得有竞争力的成功率并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成式动作模型能捕捉多样行为（同一观测与指令下的多个有效动作序列），但其迭代采样需重复网络评估，导致闭环控制推理延迟高。因此需要一个既能学习多模态动作分布、又无需迭代采样的高效目标。

Method: ESP 将策略上下文与噪声直接映射为动作块，仅需一次网络评估。其核心是用能量分数而非均方误差来训练动作头：平方误差回归以条件均值为目标，而能量分数是严格proper的，其期望值唯一地被目标分布最小化，从而为学习多模态动作分布提供有原则的目标；当模型能表示目标分布时，在总体最优处可精确恢复。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务上，ESP 取得了与流匹配基线相当的任务成功率，同时动作生成延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: 结果支持将直接分布学习作为迭代式生成机器人策略的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Generative action models based on diffusion and flow matching have been increasingly adopted in vision-language-action (VLA) policies for their ability to capture diverse behaviors, including multiple valid action sequences under the same observation and instruction. Their iterative sampling procedures, however, require repeated network evaluations to generate each action chunk, increasing inference latency in closed-loop control. We propose ESP (Energy-Score Policy), a teacher-free approach that maps policy context and noise directly to an action chunk in a single network evaluation. ESP trains the action head with the energy score rather than mean squared error. Whereas squared-error regression targets the conditional mean, the energy score is strictly proper: its expected value is uniquely minimized by the target distribution. This provides a principled objective for learning multimodal action distributions without iterative sampling, with exact recovery at the population optimum when the model can represent the target distribution. Experiments on both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate competitive task success with substantially lower action-generation latency than the flow matching baseline. These results support direct distributional learning as an efficient alternative to iterative generative robot policies.

</details>


### [56] [Seeing Through the Displaced Frame: Privileged Noise Distillation for Vision-Force Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07745)
*Ching-Hsiang Chang,Tzu-Yu Chuang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Yuan-Fu Yang,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 精度装配中的位姿误差会同时污染观测与动作坐标系，使偏移在接触前无法从本体状态识别。本文通过特权教师与闭式重标注两种监督方式，让仅用噪声状态、力觉窗口与双RGB相机的学生策略在FORGE任务和真机Franka上实现强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FORGE基准上，官方基于状态的策略在真实位姿下成功率97%~99%，但在σ=5mm位姿噪声下仅32%~60%。原因是同一估计位姿既进入观测又锚定动作坐标系，导致偏移在接触前无法从本体状态单独辨识。仅靠可部署传感不足以保证鲁棒性。

Method: 训练阶段以两种方式提供缺失的偏移信息：（1）特权教师策略在仿真中直接观测偏移；（2）对干净演示进行闭式重标注到位移坐标系。学生策略用行为克隆加一轮DAgger训练，测试时仅接收噪声状态、原始力觉窗口和两个RGB相机。另有匹配的行为克隆实验隔离鲁棒性来源。

Result: 在未修改的FORGE任务上，教师路线学生在σ=0~5mm保持92%~99%成功率，六个非特权基线降至2%~80%。匹配行为克隆实验中，无偏移访问的演示在σ=5mm仅31.5%，特权与重标注演示分别达88.7%和95.8%。零样本部署到Franka，学生在σ=5mm池化成功率83.3%，最强基于状态策略为34.4%。

Conclusion: 仅依赖可部署传感不足以应对位姿误差。实现鲁棒性需要能够编码对潜在坐标系偏移进行补偿的监督信号，特权教师与闭式重标注均能有效提供这种监督。

Abstract: Pose error in precision assembly can corrupt not only what a robot observes but also the coordinate frame in which it acts. On the FORGE benchmark, the official state-based policy succeeds in 97% to 99% of episodes with the true pose but only 32% to 60% at the benchmark's $σ=5$ mm pose-noise setting. The same estimated pose enters the observation and anchors the action frame, making the offset unidentifiable from proprioceptive state alone before contact. We supply this missing information during training in two ways. A privileged teacher observes the offset in simulation, while clean demonstrations can instead be relabelled into the displaced frame in closed form. The deployed student is trained with behaviour cloning followed by one DAgger round and receives only noisy state, a raw wrench window, and two RGB cameras at test time. On the unmodified FORGE tasks, the teacher-route student maintains 92% to 99% success across $σ=0$ to 5 mm, while six non-privileged baselines fall to 2% to 80%. A matched behaviour-cloning experiment isolates the source of this robustness. With the same student architecture, data budget, and training procedure, demonstrations generated without offset access yield only 31.5% success at $σ=5$ mm, whereas privileged and relabelled demonstrations reach 88.7% and 95.8%. Deployed zero-shot on a Franka, the student reaches 83.3% pooled success at $σ=5$ mm against 34.4% for the strongest state-based policy. Deployable sensing alone is insufficient. Robustness requires supervision that encodes compensation for the latent frame offset.Project page: https://drychang.github.io/displaced-frame/

</details>


### [57] [CoRE: Learning Collaboration-Role Experts for Decentralized Collaborative Manipulation with One Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07752)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,Zihao Li,Mingyuan Ba,Ranpeng Qiu,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRE方法，从多任务多机器人演示中学习"协作角色专家"，实现单策略去中心化协作操作，无需任务提示、身份标签或机器人间通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作需要机器人随交互进展执行互补动作。文中研究单策略去中心化协作场景：每个机器人仅依赖自身视觉观测和本体感觉运行同一策略，不使用任务提示、身份标签或机器人间消息。核心挑战在于：如何在共享参数中学习互补的团队行为，并让每个机器人从局部观测中选出恰当动作。

Method: CoRE从汇集的多任务、多机器人演示中学习协作角色专家。方法融合外观与几何信息以提供局部交互证据；采用查询条件化的交叉注意力专家，提供可适应的预测路径，并由局部路由器在每个动作块位置进行组合。训练时使用动作-专家对齐损失，基于固定输入下相对强制路由预测误差与演示进行对比，从而在无角色标签情况下监督专家选择。

Result: 在多个仿真基准上，CoRE在所评估的去中心化方法中取得最高平均性能。物理实验表明其能在多样化操作任务中实现有效协作，并对伙伴延迟与减速具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoRE通过角色专家的学习与路由，在无标签、无通信的去中心化设定下实现了互补协作行为，仿真与真实实验均验证其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collaborative manipulation requires robots to perform complementary actions as interactions unfold. We study single-policy decentralized collaboration: every robot runs the same policy from its visual observations and proprioception, without task prompts, identity labels, or inter-robot messages. The challenge is to learn complementary team behaviors within shared parameters and select appropriate actions from each robot's local observations. We introduce CoRE, which learns Collaboration-Role Experts from pooled multi-task, multi-robot demonstrations. Fused appearance and geometry provide local interaction evidence. Query-conditioned cross-attention experts provide adaptable prediction paths, which a local router combines at each action-chunk position. During training, an action-expert alignment loss supervises expert selection using relative forced-route prediction errors against demonstrations under fixed inputs, without role labels. Across simulation benchmarks, CoRE achieves the highest average performance among evaluated decentralized methods. Physical experiments demonstrate effective collaboration across diverse manipulation tasks and robustness to partner delays and slowdowns. Project page: https://aus.bot/research/core/.

</details>


### [58] [StairVLA: Stage-Aware Hierarchical Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07756)
*Shangyuan Yuan,Xinda Qi,Yujiang Pu,Wenliang Guo,Xiaobo Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StairVLA提出一种阶段感知的层次化动作生成框架，利用部分去噪动作作为高层长时程生成与局部精炼的接口，在保持性能的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散或流匹配动作头的VLA模型对去噪轨迹的处理大体是均匀的，但实际上去噪不同阶段的关注重点会自然转移：早期结合语言指令与视觉观测建立粗略动作轨迹，后期更侧重当前视觉观测进行动作对齐。这种阶段差异未被现有方法充分利用。

Method: StairVLA采用阶段感知的层次化动作生成框架。高层VLA执行早期去噪，生成可复用的长时程部分去噪动作轨迹；轻量级精炼器以更高频率运行，利用最新观测精炼局部动作块。部分去噪动作作为两部分之间的自然接口，从而摊销昂贵的高层VLA计算，同时保留频繁的闭环修正。

Result: 在LIBERO上，GR00T风格实例将平均成功率从96.5%提升至97.8%，同时将每个动作块的摊销推理延迟从115.0 ms降至44.2 ms。在两种VLA骨干、仿真基准和真实机器人任务上，StairVLA均能在保持强任务性能的同时持续降低推理成本。

Conclusion: StairVLA有效利用了扩散/流匹配去噪过程的阶段特性，通过层次化设计兼顾了长时程粗生成与高频局部精炼，在多种骨干和任务上鲁棒地实现了性能与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly rely on diffusion- or flow-matching-based action heads to generate continuous robot actions. These action heads typically process the denoising trajectory in a largely uniform manner. However, we observe that the conditioning focus naturally shifts across denoising stages: early stages combine language instructions and visual observations to establish a coarse action trajectory, whereas later stages place greater emphasis on current visual observations for action alignment. Based on this insight, we introduce StairVLA, a stage-aware hierarchical action generation framework that uses partially denoised actions as a natural interface between coarse long-horizon action generation and local refinement. A high-level VLA performs early denoising to produce a reusable long-horizon partially denoised action trajectory, while a lightweight refiner operates at a higher frequency to refine local action chunks using the latest observations. This design amortizes expensive high-level VLA computation while preserving frequent closed-loop correction. On LIBERO, our GR00T-style instantiation improves average success from 96.5% to 97.8% while reducing amortized inference latency from 115.0 ms to 44.2 ms per action chunk. More broadly, across two VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-robot tasks, StairVLA consistently reduces inference cost while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [59] [Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization for Constrained Locomotion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07772)
*Zhilin He,Xinyuan Wang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向约束运动规划的模型基几何感知生成优化方法（2GO），通过法向诱导度量整形、切空间随机滤波与CFS回缩将主动约束几何转化为可执行去噪算子，并解耦生成传输与反向随机性，在四足与人形机器人约束运动中取得更高成功率与更少违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划需同时满足碰撞避免、接触一致性、运动学可行性与支撑约束，在高维动力学和强非凸环境中极具挑战。现有模型基扩散（MBD）方法虽将轨迹优化重构为后验采样并能解析估计去噪得分，但其约束变体仍存在两点不足：(1) 缺乏任务调制的主动约束几何来引导得分方向与反向随机性；(2) 采用确定性的DDPM式反向传输，未能在不同生成传输间自适应调度。

Method: 提出模型基几何感知生成优化（2GO）。核心包括：将主动约束几何通过法向诱导度量整形、切空间随机滤波以及基于CFS的回缩操作，转化为可执行的去噪算子；并进一步通过自适应扩散与类流调度，将生成传输与反向随机性解耦。

Result: 在约束四足与人形机器人运动任务上的实验表明，2GO在离散落脚点选择与连续姿态规划中均表现优异，具有更高的成功率、更少的约束违规以及更好的执行兼容性。

Conclusion: 2GO通过几何感知约束解码与自适应生成调度，有效提升了约束运动规划的可行性与执行表现，为模型基生成式轨迹优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Constrained Locomotion Planning (CLP) for quadrupeds and humanoids, where robots must satisfy collision avoidance, contact consistency, kinematic feasibility, and support constraints, is challenging under high-dimensional dynamics and highly non-convex environments. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast trajectory optimization as posterior sampling over trajectories, using known dynamics and Monte Carlo rollouts to analytically estimate the denoising score function without demonstration learning. While constrained variants further incorporate feasibility into model-based score rollouts and show promising performance, they are still limited by (1) lacking a task-modulated active constraint geometry that shapes the score direction and reverse stochasticity, and (2) using deterministic DDPM-style reverse transport without adaptive scheduling across different generative transports. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization (2GO) for constrained locomotion, which turns active constraint geometry into executable denoising operators through normal- induced metric shaping, tangent-space stochastic filtering, and CFS-based retraction. 2GO further decouples generative transport from reverse stochasticity through an adaptive diffusion and flow-like schedule. Experiments on constrained quadruped and humanoid locomotion demonstrate strong performance in discrete foothold selection and continuous posture planning, with higher success rates, fewer violations, and improved execution compatibility.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Robot Multi-Goal Motion Planning with Stochastic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07784)
*William Schnyder,Valentin N. Hartmann,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将随机操作技能集成到基于采样的多机器人规划中，通过在多模态复合路线图上构建MDP，求解后得到可动态协调的反应式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人越来越多以群体形式在共享工作空间中执行真实任务，需要在复杂操作技能周围规划并发运动。这些技能涉及连续物理执行且可能有随机行为，导致执行时间可变、连续轨迹不确定。现有规划器要么仅限单机器人，要么依赖开环路径，要么采用事后调度而无法动态协调，存在明显空白。

Method: 将问题形式化为多模态复合路线图上的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。针对随机技能求解该MDP，从而得到一个反应式策略，使可控机器人能够根据其他机器人执行操作技能的情况动态调整自身运动；在规划阶段直接处理技能不确定性。

Result: 求解MDP得到的反应式策略使机器人可动态适应其他机器人的技能执行；在规划时直接消解技能不确定性，避免了保守基线方法的悲观性，实现了稳健、动态的多机器人协调。规划器代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了随机技能与基于采样的多机器人规划之间的差距，通过MDP建模与反应式策略实现动态协调，避免了保守方法的过度悲观，为多机器人并发操作任务提供了稳健规划方案。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in groups and share workspaces to execute real-world tasks, planning their concurrent motions around complex manipulation skills becomes essential. These skills involve continuous physical execution and may exhibit stochastic behavior, resulting in variable execution times and uncertain continuous trajectories. Existing planners either limit execution to single-robot scenarios, rely on open-loop paths, or use post-hoc scheduling that prevents dynamic coordination. In this paper, we address this gap by integrating stochastic skills into sampling-based multi-robot planning by formulating the problem as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) over a multi-modal composite roadmap. For stochastic skills, solving the MDP yields a reactive policy that allows controllable robots to dynamically adapt their motions in response to other robots' execution of manipulation skills. By resolving skill uncertainty directly at planning time, this approach avoids the pessimism of conservative baselines and unlocks robust, dynamic multi-robot coordination. Code for the planners is available at https://www.vhartmann.com/stochastic-skills.

</details>


### [61] [CUSP: CUSUM-Governed Survival Hazard Alarms at the Perception Onset for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07882)
*Inuk Kang,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CUSP，一种模型无关的运行时危险报警器，从干预终止日志中学习"感知起始点"，在五个未见场地的142个事件中检测到85个，远超最佳基线26个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航中，学习型导航虽用安全监督选路，但机器人沿该路径驶入危险时没有运行时报警。这种报警必须从现场日志学习，而人类干预会抢先阻止失败，导致失败本身从未被观察到；人类往往在危险临近时才干预，使干预标记滞后于其早期的不安全判断——这一更早的判断正是运行时报警需要检测的，但此前无方法针对它。

Method: 提出CUSP（CUSUM治理的感知起始生存模型），模型无关的运行时危险报警器，从干预终止日志中学习"感知起始点"（安全驾驶转为不安全的临界时刻），并将其与干预单独标注。视觉危险头用标注的起始点配合离散时间生存目标训练，使有、无起始点的驾驶都参与监督；再用CUSUM将预测的起始风险累积为报警。

Result: 在五个未见场地（两个自主、三个遥操作）、142个事件上评估，所有方法调至相同误报率（每小时10次）。CUSP检测到85个事件，而九个适配基线中最佳仅26个，优势来自导航模型中所有信号都无法察觉的危险。

Conclusion: CUSP通过针对"感知起始点"而非干预时刻进行监督，显著提升运行时危险报警能力，其增益来源于对常规导航信号盲区危险的检测。

Abstract: Off-road navigation exposes a robot to potentially hazardous terrain en route. Although learning-based navigation uses safety supervision to choose which path to drive, it provides no runtime alarm when the robot following that path is heading into danger. Such an alarm must be learned from field logs, where human intervention preempts the failure and the failure itself is therefore never observed. The human judges driving unsafe early but typically intervenes only once failure is clearly near, so the intervention marks that judgment late. That earlier judgment is what a runtime alarm must detect, yet no prior intervention-supervised method has targeted it. To address this problem, we introduce CUSP (CUSUM-governed Survival model of the Perception onset), a model-agnostic runtime hazard alarm that learns this moment from intervention-terminated logs. "Cusp" is a word for the point at which one state is about to turn into another, and the moment we target is exactly such a cusp: the point at which safe driving turns unsafe in a human's judgment. We call this point the perception onset and annotate it separately from the intervention. A visual hazard head is trained on the annotated onset with a discrete-time survival objective so that driving with and without an onset both supervise the head, and a CUSUM accumulates the predicted onset risk into alarms. We evaluate CUSP at five unseen sites, two autonomous and three teleoperated, with 142 events and every method tuned to the same rate of ten false alarms per hour. CUSP detected 85 events compared to 26 for the best of nine adapted baselines, and the margin comes from hazards to which every signal in the navigation model is blind.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Retargeting: Low-Latency and Robust Humanoid Whole-Body Teleoperation with Learned Atomic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07891)
*Xiayan Xu,Jiyu Yu,Xingzhou Chen,Siyi Qian,Zongyu Ma,Lilu Liu,Ling Shi,Haodong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免重定向策略，将原始人体运动单次前向映射为机器人关节指令，并借助全身运动基元码本提升对分布外观测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形全身遥操作系统依赖在线运动重定向来弥合人机形态差异，但引入延迟并可能产生物理不可行的目标；同时多样、噪声大、部分观测的人体运动常超出训练分布，导致机器人行为不稳定。

Method: 提出免重定向策略，将原始人体运动在单次前向传播中直接映射为机器人关节命令，取消在线运动学适配；学习一个全身运动基元码本，将分布外观测投影到合理的运动原型上，并从部分输入中恢复全身运动。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 的仿真与真机实验中，使用 VR、光学动捕、文本生成运动及单目视频等多种输入，所提方法在延迟与鲁棒性上优于基线。

Conclusion: 免重定向策略结合运动基元码本可在多种人体运动输入下实现低延迟、高鲁棒的全身遥操作，验证了该框架在仿真与真机上的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body teleoperation translates human motion into stable robot behavior in real time. Existing systems typically rely on online motion retargeting to bridge human--robot morphological differences, but this process adds latency and can produce physically infeasible targets. Meanwhile, diverse, noisy, and partial human-motion observations often fall outside the training distribution, potentially causing unstable robot behavior. We propose a retargeting-free policy that maps raw human motion directly to robot joint commands in a single forward pass, eliminating online kinematic adaptation. To improve robustness, we learn a codebook of full-body motion primitives that projects out-of-distribution observations onto plausible motion prototypes and recovers full-body motion from partial inputs. Experiments on a Unitree~G1 in simulation and on hardware, using virtual reality, optical mocap, text-to-motion generation, and monocular video inputs, show that our method outperforms baselines in latency and robustness.

</details>


### [63] [From Model to Prototype: Design and Motor-Flap Propulsion Control of a Twin-Wing Metamorphic UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07896)
*Anja Bosak,Dorian Erić,Ana Milas,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了变形无人机MetaMorpher的悬停控制策略，提出电机脉冲推进和舵面脉冲推进两种相位同步方法，仿真与实验证明了模型的正确性与控制稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MetaMorpher是一种兼具旋翼悬停与固定翼巡航能力的变形无人机。由于巡航配置的控制研究已相对成熟，本文聚焦于悬停模式的控制问题，基于前期工作已验证的飞行动力学模型和概念设计，探索更优的悬停控制方案。

Method: 采用两种相位同步控制策略：一是电机推进，即使电机推力与飞行器旋同步脉冲；二是舵面推进，即让翼上升降副翼的偏转与旋同步脉冲，这是本文提出的新策略。通过多种飞行实验在仿真中评估两种策略，并用物理样机对垂直动力学推进模型进行实验验证。

Result: 仿真结果验证了数学模型的正确性，并显示出稳定的参考跟踪性能；舵面推进被证明是一种轻量、解耦的悬停控制替代方案。实验测试验证了垂直动力学推进模型，在不同配置下与物理样机均表现出非常好的稳态一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的相位同步控制策略可行有效，其中舵面推进为悬停控制提供了轻量化解耦方案，仿真与实验共同验证了MetaMorpher悬停动力学模型与控制方法的正确性。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and control strategy for MetaMorpher, a metamorphic Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV), capable of both spinning-wing hover and fixed flying-wing cruise flight. Since control of the cruise configuration is comparatively well established in the literature, this paper focuses on control of the MetaMorpher in hover mode, building on the flight dynamics model and conceptual design validated in previous work. The control algorithm is implemented using one of two phase-synchronized strategies: motor propulsion, which pulses motor thrust in synchrony with the vehicle's rotation, or flap propulsion, a novel strategy that pulses the deflection of the wing-mounted elevons. We evaluate both strategies in simulation through different flight experiments. Simulation results confirm the mathematical model and show stable reference tracking, demonstrating flap propulsion as a lightweight, decoupled alternative for hover control. Experimental testing validated the vertical-dynamics propulsion model against the physical prototype, demonstrating very good steady-state agreement across different configurations.

</details>


### [64] [PACE: Stage-Consistent Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Progress-Aligned Context for Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07917)
*Yenan Chen,Junjie Shi,Lu Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Rong Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PACE，一种基于有状态记忆与进度对齐上下文的方法，用于缓解长时程机器人操作中的“阶段混淆”问题，并在多个任务上取得显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于演示条件的策略虽然为指定机器人行为提供了自然接口，但长时程操作仍面临挑战：视觉上相似的状态会在不同阶段反复出现，且演示与执行的速度可能不一致，作者将由此导致的失败模式识别为“阶段混淆”。

Method: PACE 将完整演示压缩为有序的多模态提示 token，并使用仅训练时的双边缘注意力监督来暴露演示中的潜在阶段结构。执行时，通过一个情节局部的快速权重记忆因果地编码已实现的观测-动作转移，并调制提示交叉注意力，从而为统一的扩散动作专家生成进度对齐的上下文。该方法无需测试时的阶段标签或阶段专用策略。

Result: PACE 在 LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain 上将成功率从 88.9% 提升至 94.0%，在 Spatial Combination 上从 79.1% 提升至 83.3%，在两步 Block Routing 任务上从 33.3% 提升至 73.3%。失败分析进一步表明，结构化演示对齐与因果执行记忆共同缓解了阶段混淆。

Conclusion: PACE 通过结构化演示对齐与因果执行记忆的结合，有效应对长时程操作中的阶段混淆，在多个基准任务上显著提升成功率，且不依赖测试时的阶段标签或专用策略。

Abstract: Demonstration-conditioned policies provide a natural interface for specifying robot behavior, yet long-horizon manipulation remains difficult when visually similar states recur across different stages or when demonstrations and executions proceed at different speeds. We identify the resulting failure mode as stage confusion and introduce Progress-Aligned Context for Execution (PACE), a stateful method that continually reinterprets a complete demonstration according to realized execution progress. PACE compresses the demonstration into ordered multimodal prompt tokens and uses training-only dual-edge attention supervision to expose its latent stage structure. During execution, an episode-local fast-weight memory causally encodes realized action-observation transitions and modulates prompt cross-attention, producing a progress-aligned context for a unified diffusion action expert without test-time stage labels or stage-specific policies. PACE improves success from 88.9% to 94.0% on LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain, from 79.1% to 83.3% on Spatial Combination, and from 33.3% to 73.3% on the two-step Block Routing tasks. Failure analysis further indicates that structured demonstration alignment and causal execution memory jointly mitigate stage confusion.

</details>


### [65] [OpenWAM: An Open Framework for Composable World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07922)
*Heng Yu,David D. Yuan,Juze Zhang,Changan Chen,Yao Feng,Michelle Baldonado,Steve Cousins,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OPENWAM 提出一个开放的 world-action 建模框架，用统一的因果机器人视频基座和可配置的视频-动作交互结构，系统比较不同 WAM 设计并研究反事实动力学学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action 模型同时改变视频骨干、交互结构、监督方式和推理流程，导致设计选择难以公平比较；同时动力学学习多局限于成功演示，缺少对反事实转换的利用。

Method: 基于 Wan2.2-5B，在超 10000 小时视频上做因果机器人视频预训练；通过共享 Mixture-of-Transformers 集成动作专家，支持联合、先视频后动作、先动作后视频、解耦四种生成模式；同一架构扩展到逆向与正向动力学，并引入反事实数据监督。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件和真实双臂任务上取得高成功率；因果自适应机器人视频训练将 LIBERO-Long 的 VTA 成功率从 68.4% 提升到 97.8%；冻结的局部上下文逆向模型配合反事实与演示数据在四个留出 LIBERO-90 任务上达 84.0% 平均成功率，优于全上下文 47.0% 和仅演示 21.5%；正向动力学中反事实监督降低 RGB 预测误差 34.5%，将 16 个同状态结果识别率从 21.1% 提升到 71.3%。

Conclusion: OPENWAM 为比较 WAM 交互设计提供统一测试平台，并展示了利用反事实视频数据超越成功演示来学习动力学的价值。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future prediction with robot control, yet existing systems often vary the video backbone, interaction structure, supervision, and inference procedure simultaneously, making their design choices difficult to compare. We introduce OPENWAM, an open world-action modeling framework built around a common causal robot-video foundation and configurable video-action interaction. Starting from Wan2.2-5B, we perform causal robot-video pretraining on over 10,000 hours of video, then integrate an action expert through a shared Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports joint, video-then-action, action-then-video, and decoupled generation. OPENWAM achieves high success rates on four LIBERO suites and real-world bimanual tasks; robot-video training with causal adaptation improves VTA success on LIBERO-Long from 68.4% to 97.8%. The same configurable architecture naturally extends to inverse and forward dynamics, allowing us to study how counterfactual transitions improve independently trained dynamics models beyond demonstrations alone. When only the video predictor is adapted to a new task, a frozen local-context inverse dynamics model trained on counterfactual data and demonstrations achieves 84.0% mean success across four held-out LIBERO-90 tasks, compared with 47.0% for a full-context inverse model and 21.5% for a local-context model trained only on demonstrations. For forward dynamics, counterfactual supervision reduces RGB prediction error by 34.5% and raises outcome identification from 21.1% to 71.3% among 16 same-state outcomes. OPENWAM provides a common testbed for comparing WAM interaction designs and for studying dynamics learning from video data beyond successful demonstrations.

</details>


### [66] [Adapting Vision-Language-Action Models to Unknown Visual Disruptions During Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07946)
*Ahin Lee,Jinwoo Seo,Youngsoo Jang,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALT 利用上一动作块未执行部分（leftover trajectory）作为自监督目标，实现视觉扰动下的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在执行任务时可能遭遇视觉扰动，而 VLA 策略无法预知扰动类型与发生时机，需要无需标注的测试时自适应方法。

Method: 将上一动作块未执行部分作为自监督目标，利用连续块的时间重叠对齐当前预测；通过 Transition Anchoring 在扰动起始处用扰动前的计划锚定自适应，并借助 Sequential Correction Propagation 将修正沿执行轨迹传递。监督完全来自策略自身预测，无需扰动标注、专家动作或目标域示范，并用仅在正常轨迹上校准的轻量自适应门控决定更新时机。

Result: LIBERO-10 上五种持续视觉扰动平均成功率：SmolVLA 由 43.9% 提升至 53.2%，GR00T N1.7 由 58.7% 提升至 66.0%，且基本保持正常性能；真机任务进度均值由 0.49 提升至 0.61。

Conclusion: SALT 证明 leftover trajectory 可提供有效自监督，在不依赖额外标注的前提下提升视觉扰动下的鲁棒性与真实机器人表现。

Abstract: Visual disruptions can arise while a robot is executing a task, leaving a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to respond without knowing the disruption type or timing. We introduce Self-supervised Adaptation from Leftover Trajectories (SALT), which uses the leftover trajectory, the unexecuted part of the previous action chunk, as self-supervision for test-time adaptation. Because consecutive chunks overlap in time, the leftover provides a temporally aligned target for the current prediction over the same future control interval. At the onset of a visual shift, the leftover can retain a plan formed before the corruption, so updating the policy toward it anchors the adaptation across the shift (Transition Anchoring). SALT keeps the adapted policy and regenerates the current chunk, whose leftover becomes the target at the next replan, carrying the correction forward along the execution trajectory (Sequential Correction Propagation). Supervision comes entirely from the policy's own predictions, requiring no disruption annotations, expert actions, or target-domain demonstrations, and a lightweight adaptation gate calibrated only on nominal trajectories decides when updates begin. On LIBERO-10, SALT increases average success across five persistent visual corruptions from 43.9% to 53.2% with SmolVLA and from 58.7% to 66.0% with GR00T N1.7, while largely preserving nominal performance. On a real robot, it raises task progress averaged over digital and physical disruptions from 0.49 to 0.61.

</details>


### [67] [Commit While Futures Agree: Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07949)
*Yuyan Li,Yujia Wang,Yusong Huang,Junjie Yang,Yanggang Sheng,Ziyi Shi,Wenpeng Xu,Xiaoyang Zhou,Haoang Li,Hongliang Lu,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CA³C框架，通过想象未来的共识动态决定动作块执行长度，无需重训基策略，失败率最多相对降低71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块策略需在重规划前决定执行多长的前缀，现有方法用固定长度或基于动作相似性/稳定性的自适应估计，但相似动作可导致不同未来、不同动作也可能导致相同成功结果，因而信任度应由想象未来的一致性而非动作空间相似性决定。

Method: CA³C是一种推理时框架：基于动作条件世界模型，在同一采样噪声下想象多个候选动作块的未来后果；将执行长度估计建模为贝叶斯变点推断问题；通过未来共识选择执行候选动作块，全程不修改也不重训基策略。

Result: 在多个仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务中，CA³C稳定提升多种动作分块策略，失败率相对对应基策略最多降低71.8%。

Conclusion: "imagined futures agree → commit, diverge → replan"的原则为执行长度自适应提供有效范式，作为通用推理时包装器可即插即用增强现有分块策略。

Abstract: Action-chunking policies predict multi-step control sequences, but a fundamental question remains: how much of a predicted action chunk should be committed before replanning? Existing systems typically execute a fixed-length prefix, implicitly assuming that the same execution horizon remains trustworthy across states. Some adaptive methods estimate this horizon from the similarity or stability of predicted actions. However, different actions may lead to the same successful outcome, whereas similar actions can produce different futures, suggesting that commitment should be determined by agreement among imagined futures rather than by similarity in action space. To this end, we propose Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking (CA$^3$C), an inference-time framework built on a simple principle: commit while imagined futures agree, and replan when they diverge. Without modifying or retraining the base policy, CA$^3$C uses an action-conditioned world model to imagine the future consequences of multiple candidate action chunks under the same sampling noise. Using these imagined consequences, we formulate execution-horizon estimation as a Bayesian change-point inference problem and select the execution candidate through future consensus. Across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robot manipulation tasks, CA$^3$C consistently improves diverse action-chunking policies, achieving up to a 71.8% relative reduction in failure rate over the corresponding base policies.

</details>


### [68] [IronMan: Information-Constrained Video-Action Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07961)
*Yuanshuo Zhang,Wenzhe Zhao,Zixing Lei,Bin Chen,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IronMan框架，基于信息瓶颈原理对视频-动作学习施加信息约束，抑制无关视觉细节、保留动作相关动态，在LIBERO等任务上取得高成功率与OOD鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频动作模型（VAMs）将视觉动态建模与动作生成耦合用于机器人操作，但视频表示并不天然适合动作生成：动作策略暴露于过多视觉细节会损害其泛化能力。

Method: IronMan（Information-constRained videO-actioN learning for robot MANipulation）是一个基于信息瓶颈原理的鲁棒视频-动作学习框架，核心是施加信息约束以抑制无关视觉信息、同时保留动作相关的动态线索；具体采用dynamics-aware bottleneck，将噪声大且纠缠的一步视频特征蒸馏为紧凑的世界表示。

Result: 仿真与真实实验显示良好ID性能与OOD鲁棒性且推理高效：LIBERO成功率99.0%，RoboTwin clean2clean 79.4%，优于所有对比基线；OOD场景下LIBERO-Plus成功率达79.1%，超过最强基线10.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过信息瓶颈约束压缩视觉信息、保留动作相关动态，可有效提升视频-动作模型的分布内性能与分布外鲁棒性，同时保持高效推理。

Abstract: Video Action Models (VAMs) couple visual dynamics modeling with action generation for robot manipulation. However, video representations are not naturally suited to action generation, as exposing the action policy to excessive visual detail can impair its generalization ability. Therefore, we introduce IronMan (Information-constRained videO-actioN learning for robot MANipulation), a robust video-action learning framework built on the information bottleneck principle. The core principle of this framework is to impose information constraints that suppress irrelevant visual information while preserving action-relevant dynamics cues. IronMan employs a dynamics-aware bottleneck that distills noisy, entangled one-step video features into compact world representations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance and out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness while maintaining efficient inference. IronMan achieves success rates of 99.0% on LIBERO and 79.4% on RoboTwin clean2clean, outperforming all the evaluated baselines. Under OOD shifts, IronMan achieves a success rate of 79.1% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding the strongest baseline by 10.4 percentage points. Project page: https://youngsoul0731.github.io/ironman-project-page/

</details>


### [69] [Reactive Task-Oriented Robot-Human Handovers via Generative Hypothesis Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08003)
*Carmen Scheidemann,Andreea Tulbure,Pascal Burkhardt,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出GENESIS-Handover方法，利用VLM图像生成产生任务特定的手-物交互假设，实时匹配观测到的人手姿态以推断最佳交接配置，实现面向任务的机器人-人交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类交接物体时会结合几何与语义信息（如递刀时柄朝对方）。现有面向任务的交接方法虽从几何建模发展到物体可供性，但往往忽略了对任务特定、显式手部姿态的预测。由于许多物体支持多种交互方式（如羊角锤可敲击或拔钉），这种多样性必须被建模才能实现稳健的任务导向交接。

Method: 提出GENESIS-Handover（GENErative HypotheSIS）方法，借助视觉语言模型（VLM）的图像生成能力，生成多种任务特定的手-物交互假设；将这些假设与实时观测到的人手姿态进行匹配，从而推断最合适的交接配置。方法以VLM作为合理手-物交互的先验，为未见过的物体-任务组合生成任务条件下的交接策略。

Result: 先对独立的交互提议模块进行评估，再将完整系统部署到移动机械臂上。在12名参与者、五组任务-物体对的用户研究中，83.3%的参与者认为该方法相比此前的最优方法具有更好的任务理解能力。

Conclusion: 利用VLM作为手-物交互先验，可生成任务条件下的交接策略，能够泛化到未见过的物体-任务组合，并在用户研究中优于现有最优方法，验证了显式建模任务特定手部姿态对稳健任务导向交接的重要性。

Abstract: When humans hand each other objects, they incorporate both geometric and semantic information into this process. For example, passing a knife with the handle towards the recipient, rather than the blade, is both more ergonomic and safer. Recent state-of-the-art methods for task-oriented robot-human handovers have progressed from modeling object geometry to incorporating object affordances. However, they often forgo predicting the explicit, task-specific hand poses a human selects to utilize an object. Since many objects support multiple interaction modalities, e.g., a claw hammer used to strike or pull nails, this variability must be modeled to achieve robust task-oriented handovers. To tackle this, we propose a novel approach, GENESIS-Handover (GENErative HypotheSIS), which leverages VLM image generation to produce a variety of task-specific hand-object interaction hypotheses. These hypotheses are matched in real time to the observed human hand pose, enabling inference of the most suitable handover configuration. By leveraging VLMs as priors of plausible hand-object interactions, the method produces task-conditioned handover strategies for previously unseen object-task pairs. We evaluate the standalone interaction proposal module before deploying the full system on a mobile manipulator. In a user study with 12 participants across five task-object pairs, 83.3% perceived our method to have better task understanding than the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [70] [Vector Map Quality Metrics for Contextual Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08043)
*Marie-Ngoïe Badibanga Kalenda,Philippe Bonnifait,Marie-Anne Mittet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为GOSPAM的矢量地图质量度量指标，用于从位置误差、存在性和完整性方面衡量地图差异，以支持自动驾驶中的地图更新决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全依赖持续的地图维护与可靠的质量指标。需要判断何时何地触发地图更新（如通过众包数据），以及何种条件下部署新的地图编译，因此迫切需要有效的矢量地图质量度量方法。

Method: 提出新的度量指标GOSPAM，从位置误差、存在性和完整性三个方面衡量地图差异；并在点特征和折线特征地图上进行详细仿真，分析其对偏差、假阳性、假阴性及坐标误差等常见地图退化的敏感性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，GOSPAM能够提供统一且可解释的度量，有效捕捉多种形式的地图偏差。

Conclusion: GOSPAM是汽车应用中地图质量评估的有力候选方案，可辅助地图更新触发与部署决策。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in autonomous driving requires continuous map maintenance supported by reliable quality indicators. In this context, it is crucial to identify when and where map updates should be triggered, for instance through crowdsourced data, and under which conditions a new map compilation should be deployed. This paper focuses on effective metrics for assessing the quality of vector maps and guiding such decisions. We present a new metric called GOSPAM designed to measure map discrepancies in terms of location errors, existence, and completeness. Through detailed simulations on both point and polyline feature maps, we analyze its sensitivity to common map degradation such as bias, false positives, false negatives, and coordinate errors. The results demonstrate that GOSPAM offers a unified and interpretable measure that effectively captures various forms of map deviation, making it a strong candidate for map quality assessment in automotive applications.

</details>


### [71] [Navigation with RF Cues: Embodied Perception Action under Multipath Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08105)
*Wenlihan Lu,Tianshun Li,Liuqing Yang,Shijian Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无先验地图与未知目标坐标的智能工厂巡检导航问题，构建了Habitat Sionna RT基准，并提出不确定性感知的多模态导航框架，在未知场景中相对最强基线提升SR 18.2%、SPL 11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂巡检要求机器人无需先验地图或已知目标坐标即可抵达相连设备。目标RF信号可在遮挡或光照不足时提供方向线索以补充视觉观测，但多径传播会扭曲这些线索，使其难以从瞬时RF测量中推断目标真实方向。为此需要构建相应基准并研究此条件下的导航方法。

Method: 首先构建Habitat Sionna RT基准，利用详细场景几何与指定材质属性，针对机器人动作生成对齐的视觉与RF观测。在此基准上提出不确定性感知多模态导航框架，从RF、视觉与位姿观测的历史序列中联合估计目标方向及其不确定性，并将这些估计与视觉上下文一同用于动作选择。

Result: 在未知场景实验中，相比最强评测基线，成功率（SR）相对提升18.2%，路径长度加权成功率（SPL）相对提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 所构建的基准与不确定性感知多模态框架可有效支撑无先验地图、目标坐标未知条件下的RF-视觉融合导航，并在未知场景中取得优于基线的性能。

Abstract: Smart factory inspection requires robots to reach connected equipment without a prior map or known target coordinates. Radio frequency (RF) signals from the target can provide directional cues to complement visual observations when occlusion or poor lighting limits target detection. However, multipath propagation can distort these cues, making it difficult to infer the target's true direction from instantaneous RF measurements. To enable navigation research under these conditions, we first construct a Habitat Sionna RT benchmark that uses detailed scene geometry and assigned material properties to generate aligned visual and RF observations in response to robot actions. Building on this benchmark, we propose an uncertainty aware multimodal navigation framework that jointly estimates target direction and its uncertainty from a history of RF, visual, and pose observations. These estimates inform action selection alongside visual context. Experiments in unseen scenes show relative improvements of 18.2% in success rate (SR) and 11.5% in success weighted by path length (SPL) over the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [72] [Reactive Exploration of Unknown Environments for Redundant Robots using Virtual Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08110)
*Alessio Canzolino,Omar Faris,Alessandro De Blasi,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向冗余机械臂的主动探索方法，结合眼在手相机配置，通过新型体素评分策略实时选取目标体素，并使用虚拟模型控制实现全身反应式避障，在受限与开阔环境中实现超过90%的建图覆盖率且无碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限、遮挡及部分已知空间中的机器人操作面临挑战：机械臂整体位姿需在满足紧几何约束的同时避开新发现的障碍物，传统方法难以兼顾安全性与探索效率。

Method: 提出眼在手相机配置下的冗余机械臂主动探索方法：基于机器人当前状态与期望信息增益设计新的评分方法，直接从未探索空间中选择目标体素；采用虚拟模型控制驱动机器人安全移动至目标体素，保证柔顺性并实现全身反应式避障，无需重新规划路径。

Result: 在受限与开阔环境中的仿真及真实机器人实验表明方法有效，在所有测试环境中建图覆盖率超过90%，耗时少于120秒，且未与障碍物发生碰撞。

Conclusion: 该主动探索框架无需路径重规划即可在复杂受限空间中实现高效、安全的实时探索与建图，验证了信息增益评分与虚拟模型控制结合的有效性。

Abstract: The exploration of confined, occluded, and partially known spaces poses significant challenges in robotic manipulation. The overall pose of the robotic arm must be carefully controlled to respect tight geometric constraints while avoiding newly discovered obstacles. We address this problem by proposing an active exploration approach for redundant robotic arms with an eye-in-hand camera configuration. Our approach navigates and acquires information in real-time based on a novel scoring method that directly selects a target voxel from the unexplored space using the robot's current state and expected information gain. To move the robot safely toward the target voxel, we utilize Virtual Model Control, which guarantees compliance and enables whole-body reactive obstacle avoidance without the need for path replanning. Simulated and real-robot experiments in both confined and open environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving over $90\%$ mapping coverage across all tested environments in under $120$ seconds without colliding with obstacles.

</details>


### [73] [Energy-Aware Path Following: Comparative Analysis of Reinforcement Learning and NMPC for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08112)
*Mohamed Sabaa,Mostafa Emam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在统一Frenet坐标系运动学车辆模型下，结合含再生制动的VT-CPEM能量模型，对比分析了NMPC、PPO、增益调度Ackermann状态反馈(PID-SF)和Stanley几何控制器四种路径跟踪策略，并验证了PPO策略的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径跟踪控制存在双目标优化困境：既要最小化对参考路径的偏差，又要保持平滑的速度曲线。后者对电动车尤为重要，因为通过再生制动回收能量可延长其有限的续航里程，而这一特性在现有文献中研究不足。

Method: 在统一的Frenet坐标系运动学车辆模型下，采用经校验且显式包含再生制动的VT-CPEM能量模型，实现并对比四种控制器：非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)、近端策略优化(PPO)、增益调度Ackermann状态反馈基线(PID-SF)和Stanley几何基线。为满足实时性要求，NMPC采用JIT编译的CasADi实现；PPO在传统直线和S形曲线赛道上训练，随后将未修改的策略迁移至未见赛道。

Result: PPO策略成功迁移至未见赛道，包括ISO 3888-1车道变换、chicane、随机生成的参数化样条以及±3°坡道。此外，策略零样本迁移至带线性轮胎的动力学单轨车辆模型时具有可接受的初始性能，经简短微调后得到优化。这表明PPO可便捷迁移至更复杂的车辆模型。

Conclusion: 本文展示了所开发控制器的性能分析，并讨论了未来工作的方向。

Abstract: Path-following control strategies typically follow the bi-objective optimization dilemma: minimizing deviations from a reference path while maintaining smooth speed profiles. The latter objective is especially relevant for Electric Vehicles (EVs), since their limited driving range can be extended by recovering energy through regenerative braking, a feature that has not yet been sufficiently studied in the literature. In this work, we perform a comparative analysis of four controllers under one common Frenet frame-based kinematic vehicle model, utilizing a validated energy model (VT-CPEM) with explicit regenerative braking. Herein, we implement the following controllers: Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), gain-scheduled Ackermann state-feedback baseline (PID-SF), and a Stanley geometric baseline. To satisfy real-time requirements, we implement the NMPC using JIT-compiled CasADi. Moreover, we train the PPO using traditional straight and S-curve tracks, after which we successfully transfer the unmodified policy to unseen tracks, including: an ISO 3888-1 lane-change, a chicane, randomly-generated parameterized-splines, and a $\pm3^\circ$ graded road. In addition, the policy transfers to a dynamic single-track vehicle model with linear tires, zero-shot with an acceptable initial performance, which was optimized after brief fine-tuning. Thereby, we demonstrate that our PPO is readily transferable to more comprehensive vehicle models. We conclude with a performance analysis of developed controllers and discuss ideas for future work.

</details>


### [74] [AutodidactWAM: Cross-Modal Self-Distillation from Generated Video to Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08119)
*Sergei Kurchev,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对世界-动作模型在新机器人上的"视频-动作不对称"问题，提出 AutodidactWAM：用无遥操作训练的手部姿态估计器在生成视频上恢复动作，并通过偏好监督+监督拟合+轨迹锚定的混合目标微调动作层，显著提升抓取与整体任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（如 Cosmos 3）能从观测和指令联合生成未来视频与机器人动作，但用轻量 LoRA 微调将其适配到未曾见过的机器人（Unitree G1 人形机器人 + BrainCo 五指手）时，暴露出明显的视频-动作不对称：生成视频看起来任务执行合理，但协同生成的动作却系统性定位错误。作者希望解决这一动作失准问题。

Method: 提出 AutodidactWAM：先训练一个无需遥操作的手部姿态估计器，再通过逆运动学在模型生成的视频上运行以恢复动作估计；将恢复的动作与原生动预测配对作为偏好目标，只微调与动作相关的层，同时教师强制（teacher-forcing）生成视频。对比监督式重标注与 Diffusion-DPO 的整流流适配，并进一步提出结合偏好监督、监督目标拟合与笛卡尔轨迹锚定（DTW）的混合目标（DPO+SFT+DTW）。一次具身适配后，自蒸馏无需额外任务专用遥操作。

Result: 闭环节真机试验分三阶段（pre-grasp、grasp、pick-and-place）：原生动作成功率约 17%、10%、7%，在留出物体上更差；恢复动作门控达约 75%、47%、42%。混合目标 DPO+SFT+DTW 表现最佳：训练物体 Oreo 上 pre-grasp 90%、全任务 20%；留出物体上 80%、30%。纯 Flow-DPO 尽管验证偏好准确率为 1.000，成功率却为 0%，说明增益来自训练目标的组合而非单一对比目标。

Conclusion: 世界-动作模型存在视频生成合理但动作失准的不对称问题；通过基于视频的动作恢复与多目标联合微调可显著改善真机成功率，且一次具身适配后无需额外遥操作。关键结论是：仅靠对比式偏好目标（Flow-DPO）无效，必须结合监督拟合与轨迹锚定等多种训练目标才能获得实际收益。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) such as Cosmos 3 jointly generate future video and robot actions from an observation and instruction. Adapting one such model with a lightweight LoRA fine-tune to a previously unseen robot, a Unitree G1 humanoid with five-fingered BrainCo hands, exposes a video-action asymmetry: the video renders plausible task executions, while the co-generated action is systematically mis-targeted. We evaluate closed-loop real-robot trials at three cumulative stages: pre-grasp, grasp, and pick-and-place. The native action succeeds only approximately 17%, 10%, and 7% of the time, respectively, and performs worse on held-out objects. We propose AutodidactWAM, a hand-pose estimator trained without teleoperation, followed by inverse kinematics, that runs on the model's generated video to recover action estimates. Paired with the native prediction, these recovered actions provide preferred targets for fine-tuning only the action-related layers, while the generated video is teacher-forced. We compare supervised relabeling with a rectified-flow adaptation of Diffusion-DPO. After one-time embodiment adaptation, self-distillation requires no additional task-specific teleoperation.
