It also completes all forest flights with up to 9.5 m/s speed without collisions, and achieves 86 % flight success rate under a more aggressive speed profile where a state-of-the-art planner has only 26 % success rate. The real-world deployment showed reliable execution flying up to 5.5 m/s in an unknown cluttered environment.

</details>


### [112] [EgoLAP: Learning from Egocentric Human Data through Language-Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08726)
*Lihan Zha,Shresth Grover,Tenny Yin,Samuel M. Bateman,Hengkai Pan,Mengchao Zhang,Aykut Onol,Allen Z. Ren,Dhruv Shah,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLAP通过共享的语言化动作思维链，将人类与机器人轨迹联合预训练，跨越具身差异，实现更有效的人类经验向机器人控制的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身人类数据可助力机器人学习规模化，避免依赖昂贵的机器人示范；但人类与机器人的具身差异使原始人类轨迹难以直接作为控制监督信号。作者洞察到：低层动作虽与具身相关，但其背后的运动意图蕴含跨人机可迁移的任务相关结构。

Method: 提出EgoLAP，一种VLA预训练框架，通过共享的、基于语言的动作思维链，联合学习人类与机器人轨迹。方法将运动意图表达为结构化、时间抽象的语言动作，并与基于场景几何、物理和物体可供性的运动级推理相配对。

Result: 在大量真实与仿真实验中，EgoLAP比替代动作表示更有效地将人类经验迁移至机器人控制，达到80.1%的平均真实任务进度，为替代动作表示的2.3倍性能提升；运动级推理也优于融合子任务、物体框与视觉轨迹的复合推理格式。

Conclusion: 以语言化动作思维链表达跨具身的运动意图，是一种有效的人类到机器人知识迁移途径，优于直接使用动作表示及复合推理格式。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offer a path to scaling robot learning beyond costly robot demonstrations, yet the embodiment gap makes raw human trajectories a poor supervisory target for control. Our key insight is that, although low-level actions are embodiment-specific, their underlying motion intent can capture task-relevant structure that transfers across humans and robots. We introduce EgoLAP, a VLA pre-training framework that jointly learns from human and robot trajectories through a shared language-based action chain-of-thought. EgoLAP expresses motion intent as structured, temporally abstracted language actions and pairs them with motion-level reasoning grounded in scene geometry, physics, and object affordances. Across extensive real-world and simulated experiments, EgoLAP transfers human experience to robot control more effectively than alternative action representations and reaches 80.1% mean real-world task progress, a 2.3x performance gain over alternative action representations. Motion-level reasoning also outperforms a composite reasoning format that combines subtask, object-box, and visual-trace reasoning.

</details>


### [113] [Towards an Extensible Benchmark for Spoken Dialogue with Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08733)
*Casey Kennington,Ross Mead,Saad Elbeleidy,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于评估人机口语对话的基准测试，并介绍实时通信框架Retico以支持机器人语音对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型虽可作为人与机器人之间的即插即用接口，但在语音、对话、快速交互与协作场景下仍面临重要挑战。

Method: 提出一个社区可用的基准测试，用于探索机器人与人类之间常见的口语对话要素（如澄清请求、打断、具身信号如点头或面部线索、时间约束），并提出使用实时通信框架Retico以满足机器人实现口语对话的技术需求。

Result: 论文为愿景与框架提出类工作，主要阐述基准设计思路及Retico框架的适用性，未给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 希望通过该基准推动人机交互多个重要方面的研究，并扩展至更广泛的研究社区关注的问题。

Abstract: Language models provide a plug-and-play interface between humans and robots, but important challenges remain when speech, dialogue, fast interaction, and collaboration are required. We propose a benchmark for the community to use as a way to explore common spoken dialogue artifacts between robots and humans, including requests for clarification, interruptions, embodied signals (e.g., head nods or facial cues), and time constraints. We also explain our vision to extend the benchmark for other aspects of human-robot interaction that are important to the larger research community. To facilitate the benchmark, we further propose using \textit{Retico}, a real-time communication framework that fulfills important technical requirements to enable robots to have spoken dialogue capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [PhoneBot: A Low-Cost Open Humanoid Robot Platform Reusing Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08737)
*Ruochen Hou,Quanyou Wang,Daniel Koh,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhoneBot：用智能手机作为感知与计算单元的低成本开源人形机器人平台


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人应用于教育与研究受限于硬件成本高、感知系统复杂、算力需求大。

Method: 以商用智能手机为核心感知与计算单元，利用其IMU、摄像头、无线通信与板载算力；下身为13个低成本执行器驱动的模块化结构，躯干安装手机承担感知、控制计算与交互；设计机械结构、软件架构与实时通

Result: 实验验证了稳定行走、感知驱动交互，并可直接用市售消费级智能手机部署。

Conclusion: PhoneBot以全开源的软硬件设计，为教育、研究与快速原型提供低成本、可复现的平台。

Abstract: The adoption of humanoid robots in education and research remains limited by high hardware costs, complex sensing systems, and substantial computational requirements. This paper presents PhoneBot, a low-cost, open-source humanoid robot platform that repurposes commodity smartphones as its primary sensing and computing unit. By using a smartphone's integrated inertial measurement unit (IMU), camera, wireless connectivity, and onboard processing capabilities, PhoneBot reduces hardware costs and simplifies the system architecture. The robot combines a modular lower-body structure driven by 13 low-cost actuators with a torso-mounted smartphone that supports perception, control computation, and user interaction. We describe the mechanical design, software architecture, and real-time communication framework that support stable locomotion and capabilities including vision-based human following, conversational interaction, filming, and mobile telepresence. Experimental evaluations demonstrate reliable walking, perception-driven interaction, and straightforward deployment using off-the-shelf consumer smartphones. With fully open-source hardware and software designs, PhoneBot provides an affordable, reproducible platform for education, research, and rapid prototyping. More details are available at https://phonebot.dev.

</details>


### [115] [LBA-CBF: Rapidly Adaptive Safety Filters via Parallel Dynamics Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08765)
*Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Timeea-Andreea Radu,Hassan Almubarak,Henry Z. Liao,Michael Görner,Francesco Maurelli,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF)，通过在短回看窗口内用预测误差对候选动力学模型库排序，并对与最优模型误差容差内的所有模型强制高阶CBF条件，实现在突发未测工况变化下的安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF依赖假设的动力学模型对控制命令进行认证，当发生突发、不可测的工况变化时，证书可能恰好于最关键的安全时刻失效。现有自适应或鲁棒方法需要切换模型或连续参数化估计器，难以应对非线性未知参数与突变工况。

Method: LBA-CBF在短回看窗口内按近期预测误差对有限候选动力学库排序，对误差在最优模型容差范围内的每个模型强制高阶CBF条件，从而在最佳拟合自适应与全库鲁棒滤波之间连续插值；动力学可非线性依赖未知参数，无需切换模型或连续参数化估计器。

Result: 证明了只要保留一个代表安全的候选模型，任何可行滤波输入都满足真实CBF条件。四旋翼仿真中面对突然风向反转和未知载荷，LBA-CBF在所有随机初始条件下均安全并到达目标，媲美oracle，而自适应与鲁棒基线成功率仅0-88%。控制回路内可运行多达250,000个模型的模型库；Crazyflie 2.1与F1TENTH实验验证了对风、载荷释放及变化轮胎-路面摩擦的适应性。

Conclusion: LBA-CBF通过回看误差排序与容差内多模型CBF约束，提供了应对突发未测工况变化的安全认证方法，兼顾自适应与鲁棒性，并在仿真与真实平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) certify commands through an assumed dynamics model, so an abrupt, unmeasured regime change can undermine the certificate exactly when safety matters most. We present Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF), which rank a finite bank of candidate dynamics by recent prediction error over a short look-back window and enforce the high-order CBF condition against every model within a tolerance of the best, spanning best-fit adaptation to full-bank robust filtering. The dynamics may depend nonlinearly on the unknown parameters, and no switching model or continuously parameterized estimator is required. We prove that any feasible filtered input satisfies the true CBF condition whenever a safety-representative candidate is retained. In quadrotor simulation with abrupt wind reversals and an unknown payload, LBA-CBF is safe and reaches the goal from all random initial conditions, matching an oracle, while adaptive and robust baselines achieve 0-88% success. Banks of up to 250,000 models run inside the control loop, and Crazyflie 2.1 and F1TENTH experiments demonstrate adaptation to wind, payload release, and varying tire-road friction. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [116] [DepthWorld: 3D World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08780)
*Jai Bardhan,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DROID-3D标定数据集与DepthWorld世界模型，通过联合预测多视角RGB和深度，使视频世界模型获得忠实的3D几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视频的世界模型仅在RGB上训练，逐帧看似正确但无法组合成一致的3D世界，而机器人策略评估、改进和规划都依赖忠实的3D几何。缩小这一差距需要两方面进展：大规模操作任务的3D监督，以及能在不破坏强预训练视频先验的前提下吸收该监督的架构。

Method: 提出标定流程，将学习到的立体深度与联合因子图结合，汇集同一物理机器人采集的所有episode，联合恢复其共享运动学参数与逐场景外参；应用于DROID数据集得到DROID-3D，提供密集度量深度和重标定多视角外参。随后训练DepthWorld，一个基于Stable Video Diffusion的世界模型，通过空间隐变量分块联合预测多视角RGB和深度，并保持预训练VAE不变。

Result: DROID-3D在90%的episode上外部相机重投影误差小于0.7像素。在相同训练预算下，深度监督使RGB预测相比纯RGB基线提升+1.48 dB PSNR，同时产出准确的度量深度用于下游几何推理。

Conclusion: 深度监督既能提升RGB预测质量，又能提供准确度量深度，为机器人世界模型实现一致的3D几何理解提供了数据与架构两方面的可行路径。

Abstract: World models offer a data-driven alternative to traditional simulators for robotics, with applications spanning policy evaluation, improvement, and planning. All of these uses depend on faithful 3D geometry, yet current video-based world models are trained on RGB alone and produce rollouts that look correct frame-by-frame but do not compose into a consistent 3D world. Closing this gap requires progress on two fronts: large-scale 3D supervision for manipulation, and an architecture that can absorb it without disturbing strong pretrained video priors. We introduce a calibration pipeline that combines learned stereo depth with a joint factor graph, pooling all episodes collected from the same physical robot to recover its shared kinematic parameters alongside per-scene extrinsics. Applied to the DROID dataset, this yields DROID-3D, a calibrated 3D dataset providing dense metric depth and recalibrated multi-view extrinsics (achieving <0.7 px reprojection error on 90% of episodes for external cameras). We then train DepthWorld, a Stable Video Diffusion-based world model that jointly predicts multi-view RGB and depth via spatial latent tiling, leaving the pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) unchanged. Depth supervision improves RGB prediction itself by +1.48 dB PSNR over an identical RGB-only baseline at equal training budget, while simultaneously yielding accurate metric depth for downstream geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [PEARS: Physical-Prior-Guided Efficient Adaptation via Failure Reasoning and Diffusion Steering for Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08784)
*Kun Song,Yiming Wang,Yilin Chen,Tianyi Ding,Jiaxin Tian,Tianqi Gong,Daolin Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PEARS，一种物理先验引导的混合强化学习框架，通过VLM力推理与触觉条件扩散引导，实现预训练策略样本高效在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在部署中的分布外条件下性能大幅下降，需要真实交互后训练；但基于RL的后训练通常需要大量环境交互，在操作任务中每次试验缓慢、昂贵甚至具破坏性，因此需要样本高效的在线适应方法。

Method: PEARS包含两部分：一是物理引导力推理（PFR）模块，在每个回合后利用VLM中编码的物理先验，从视觉结果与触觉交互历史诊断失败并更新任务适配的接触力边界，由高频混合力-位置控制器在接触时执行这些边界；二是触觉条件扩散引导强化学习，通过调整冻结流匹配策略的潜在噪声，在不更新基座模型的情况下修正自由空间运动与接触时机误差。

Result: 仿真中，PEARS相比最强单任务基线将成功率提升12.4-37.4个百分点；相比最快基线，达到特定成功率阈值所需交互回合数最多减少53.2%。真实世界实验中，在白板擦除任务上成功率达95%，在移液枪液体吸取任务上达90%。

Conclusion: 将PFR模块与策略引导相结合可加速适应并减少昂贵交互，表明物理先验与触觉反馈联合引导是提升预训练操作策略在线适应效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Pretrained robotic policies can suffer substantial performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions encountered during deployment, motivating post-training through real-world interaction. However, reinforcement-learning (RL)-based post-training typically requires substantial environment interactions, a burden that is especially significant in manipulation, where each trial can be slow, costly, or destructive. Therefore, we present PEARS, a physics-prior-guided hybrid RL framework for sample-efficient online adaptation of pretrained policies with tactile feedback. After each episode, its physics-guided force reasoning (PFR) module uses physical priors encoded in a vision-language model (VLM) to diagnose failures from the visual outcome and tactile interaction history and update task-appropriate contact-force bounds. A high-frequency hybrid force-position controller then enforces these bounds during contact. Complementarily, tactile-conditioned diffusion steering reinforcement learning adjusts the latent noise of the frozen flow-matching policy to correct errors in free-space motion and contact timing without updating the base model. In simulation, PEARS improves success rates by 12.4-37.4 percentage points over the strongest per-task baselines. PEARS also reduces the number of interaction episodes required for a certain success threshold by up to 53.2% relative to the fastest baseline. In real-world experiments, PEARS achieves success rates of 95% on Whiteboard Erasing and 90% on Pipette Liquid Aspiration. These results show that combining the PFR module with policy steering can accelerate adaptation while reducing costly interactions. The project website is available at https://song-kun.github.io/pears.

</details>


### [118] [QF3: Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08789)
*Chung Min Kim,Brent Yi,David McAllister,Hongsuk Choi,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Jinkun Cao,Ken Goldberg,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QF3 是一种在线 off-policy 流策略强化学习算法，通过流匹配加过滤后的 critic 动作梯度训练流策略，实现从零学习人形运动策略并零样本迁移到硬件，训练速度比 FPO++ 快 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略已成为从示范中学习机器人行为的标准方法，但强化学习在改进预训练流策略或通过交互从零学习方面仍然关键。现有方法（如 on-policy 的 FPO++）训练效率低，难以从零训练人形运动策略并迁移到硬件。

Method: 提出 QF3（Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients）：1）用流匹配加 critic 的动作梯度训练流策略，梯度通过流输出的一步预测反传；2）为保证 critic 与该预测可靠，仅对保持在回放动作附近维度的动作维度施加 critic 梯度；3）配合高吞吐 off-policy 训练方案。

Result: 首次实现 off-policy 流 RL 从零训练人形运动策略并零样本迁移到硬件；人形运动和动作跟踪策略训练比 FPO++ 快 10 倍（wall-clock）；在 ABC-Sim 和 Robomimic 任务上成功微调预训练流式操作策略。

Conclusion: QF3 既能从零学习机器人策略，也能优化从示范中获得的策略，兼顾了流策略的表达能力与 off-policy RL 的高效性。

Abstract: Flow policies have become a standard policy class for learning robot behaviors from demonstrations, but reinforcement learning is still critical for improving pre-trained flow policies or learning them from scratch through interaction. We introduce QF3 (Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients), an online off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy with flow matching plus the critic's action gradient, backpropagated through a one-step prediction of the flow's output. To keep updates where the critic and this prediction are reliable, QF3 applies the critic gradient only to action dimensions that stay near the replay action. To our knowledge, QF3 is the first off-policy flow RL method to train humanoid locomotion policies from scratch and transfer them zero-shot to hardware. Paired with a high-throughput off-policy training recipe, it trains humanoid locomotion and motion-tracking policies with a 10x wall-clock speedup over FPO++, a recent on-policy flow RL method. We further apply QF3 to fine-tune pretrained flow-based manipulation policies on both ABC-Sim and Robomimic tasks. These results suggest that QF3 can both learn robot policies from scratch and refine those acquired from demonstrations. Website: https://qf3-rl.github.io/

</details>
